This DEC states them. It does not satisfy any of them.
   13. **Truncated-log rule for the H5 a2 reads** (repin ruling Q2). The registration record of an a2 book discloses: the corrected truncation rates (the ruling's own: WSOL-only PumpSwap transactions with truncated logs 209 / 5,771 (3.62%) post vs 11 / 2,723 (0.40%) pre in its sample; 17 / 5,579 (0.30%) post without the one pool), the one-pool, one-hour episode (384 CPI-only WSOL PumpSwap SellEvents in pool `34tiphuqXUgkH9c2KCrFqCiyuhz58HEbhQBk61ub46bU`), and that `records_from_logs` cannot see CPI-only events. Before any outcome is opened, each a2 read runs a counts-only audit that lists the counted attempts whose pool had any successful transaction with `Log truncated` between entry and exit (`getSignaturesForAddress(pool)` and `getTransaction` meta). If more than 5% of counted attempts are affected (the exclusion cap's level), the read is NOT_DECIDABLE. Otherwise the count is disclosed. The rule is outcome-blind and written before any a2 clock. It does not exclude attempts from the count.
   14. **Disclosures that go with this rule** (quant-proof's comments on PRs #778 and #780; each is stated plainly and none is a pass).
      - **PumpSwap write enumeration is unobserved live and fails closed.** Until it works on the RPC in use, a PumpSwap redeploy yields an ERROR and a hard halt at the bound of item 1.6: a FALSE halt, never a false pass. Exhausting the call cap is the same. Optionally `A3_ENUM_ENV_FILE` (a Helius archival RPC env file, read by the tool, never printed, used ONLY for the write enumeration) can be set; job #1312 did not run that route (the file was not readable by the job user), and 0 Helius credits were used so far. If the scheduled job sets it, the records PR and the daily note report the credits used per run (which unit, how many credits, what got faster), per CLAUDE.md.
      - **QP N-1, fail-open for two events.** `CompleteEvent` (it rides the completing transaction, not the migrate transaction) and `PostCompleteBuyEvent` (synthetic migrations; none seen) are not covered by any sample. A redeploy that changes only their layout PASSES the check. Their trailing bytes are unobserved. `WithdrawEvent` is pinned at 8 on the ruling, not on observation (never seen in a sample). Before an a2 clock either a CompleteEvent sample (the completing transactions of the K migrate mints) is added to the tool, or this stays as disclosed.
      - **Judgement pins, one sample each:** `max_capped_share` 0.05 (observed 6 of 411 Buy/Sell events, 1.5%) and `min_init_boost_in_migrate_sample` 10 (observed 23). They are named as judgement calls, not derived. The tight pins (trailing set, IDL hashes, BOOST +/-20% against the monitor pins) can cause false halts only.
      - **Pins come from one post-redeploy migrate sample and one log sample** taken after the first 10-19 write of the redeploy they judge (the log sample is between the two writes). The trailing 8 bytes ARE new on 2026-10-19 (#780 section 5(a): 0 trailing bytes on 5,148 events in slots 458763000-458878000). The field's meaning was not examined.
      - **Deadline costs** are those of item 1.6: with one run a day a redeploy found at the next run always times out; one missed run times out every pending write.
      - A later redeploy that starts creating base mints with Token-2022 transfer fees, without changing event layouts, is not caught by the A3 check (fail-open, disclosed; gates nothing; a tool check is recommended later, not required).
      - **Not exercised live:** the whole job on fast-0, the MiScusi schedule, a FAIL on live data, and a real redeploy after merge. The dead-man depends on the schedule running.
2. **P0 replacement** (a3 ruling Q6 S1 to S6; replaces the drop rule of Amendment 3 item 1 for books registered after the merge; applies to every arena book, H5 or not). The rest of Amendment 3 item 1 (P0 as a pre-declared structure-only count, the fixed-first rule text, the outcome-blind tool, the record, the order, the records PR) stands.
   1. **Floor.** P0 stays a structure-only count on at least 3 full closed UTC days at the registration-candidate sha, before the clock. It decides two things only. A book is not registered if P0 averages below 2.0 would-be attempts a day, or if any P0 day has 0. The hard-gap and `data_loss` > 1 h rule of Amendment 3 item 1 stays: a P0 window that fails it is NOT_DECIDABLE and the book is not registered on it. The old rule (fewer than 20 a day, or any day under 10) no longer drops a book.
   2. **Horizon.** The book's horizon is `H = min(90, max(14, ceil(150 / rate)))` days, with `rate` the P0 would-be attempts a day (that is 1.5 x 100 / rate). H is pinned in the `bots.json` line before the clock and is never extended. It is further capped by any end-seal or window end that applies to the book (for the H5 a2 books, the 2026-11-06T00Z arena end-seal or its successor).
   3. **Read.** DEC-031 §2's read stays: the first 00:00Z boundary with at least 100 counted attempts on at least 5 UTC dates, counted by the outcome-blind decision-timestamp tool. If that boundary has not come by clock + H, the book ENDS NOT_DECIDABLE at clock + H, final for the `bot_id`, counts only. There is no early read on fewer than 100 attempts and no extension. There is no re-registration of the same rule to continue it; a re-registration of the same rule "to continue" is a new `bot_id`, adds to N, and discloses the earlier end.
   4. **A cap, not a target.** At 2 a day, 100 attempts take about 50 days and H is 75 days. Regime drift and structure changes accrue over that time. A slow book also spends more redeploys inside its window, and each record-only redeploy is disclosed at its read (item 1.5).
   5. **N and c.** Every registered book counts in N from its clock, including books that end at H as NOT_DECIDABLE (Amendment 3 item 3). Books turned away at the floor are disclosed and not counted in N, as P0 drops are today. A lower floor means more registered books and a larger N, so the plain line 1 - 0.95^N rises (40% at N = 10, 79% at N = 30). Parallel low-frequency books are welcome, but each costs 1 in N, so they are counted before they are registered. c changes only through screen passes (unchanged). Slow books are read late, so under the running c of Amendment 3 item 2 a late confirmation faces a smaller 0.05/c. Each read says so.
   6. **Confirmation length.** A low-frequency book's confirmation also needs at least 100 further attempts on at least 5 further dates, so a book at 2 a day needs about 100 days from the clock to a confirmed screen pass. Its registration says so, so that nobody plans live money on a shorter path.
   7. **Books dropped under the old rule** (fewer than 20 a day or any day under 10) are NOT revived by this amendment. Each needs a new registration under items 2.1 and 2.2, with the old drop disclosed.

Unchanged by this amendment: the promotion gate and its thresholds (100 out-of-sample trades, at least 5 UTC dates with a majority positive, lower 90% CI bound of mean SOL per trade above 0, total positive after removing the top 3 trades, under the flat 15% and the pressure-fail models at slope scale 1); §2's deciding cell, its one read per `bot_id`, its outcome-blind read instant and step 4's 100 attempts on 5 further dates at 0.05/c; the 5% exclusion cap; Amendments 1 to 3 (including the rent of Amendment 2 and Amendment 3 item 5, no second frozen copy); every clock already registered; the a1 books and their end under the unchanged A3 rule; EXP-024 and the DEC-024 canary rules; §3 and the seals; the scorer `tools/arena_h5_score.py` at sha256 `dfe7f018601633921f06668fd1af3aaff7b87bf00f321d1540e10b41f8ab6006` (see item 1.11). No book has cleared the promotion gate, and nothing here is a profit claim.


### Amendment 5 (first written 2026-10-21T23:38Z, revised 2026-10-21T23:43Z, from `date -u` on mal-research-0; before the clock of the book it covers and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)

This turns quant-proof's ruling `ARTIFACTS/lab/qp-ruling-1022-q50.md` (repo copy, sha256 `b82592154cb79035e74eeab2f23f80d8d4d84666b641726ebced6431971501d1`; the source `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt24/l3-boost/QP-RULING-1022-q50.md` is sha256 `a0dcf39100d30b944544955981e7452e4449d5018a942d30a35a4c3a2057cac7` and the copy is unchanged below its 2-line header) sections 3, 4 and 5 and GO items G5 and G6 into DEC text. It does not change the ruling's substance. No arena tape, ledger, `bots.json` line, shadow or canary output, outcome, fill, exit or P&L was opened to write it, and no count or replay was run. **Nothing here says any book makes money, and no book has cleared the promotion gate.** The Q50 book has no known edge: Q* = 50 is a discarded discovery cell.
- Takes effect on merge, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict on the PR, and it must merge before the Q50 book's registration and clock. The rule block and the observation scope are [EXP-024](../EXP/EXP-024-h5-boostfloor-part1-prereg.md) Amendment 10 (rule file `EXP/EXP-024-RULE-Q50.md`). The flag PR, the Q50 scoring wrapper and the equivalence job are separate PRs (QP G2, G4). This note satisfies G5 and the declaration half of G6 only.

1. **P0 stand-in (a declared departure; QP section 4).**
   1. **The departure.** Amendment 3 item 1, kept by Amendment 4 item 2, makes P0 a count by "the book's rule module at its registration-candidate sha". It is `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt24/l3-boost/scripts/l3_count.py` (sha256 `c45bfbfa98f06c561e1bf302e5cc318580bae21323f13d4ce9cdc38626a7424d`) with **one line changed**, `PATH_RE` (line 17), which at c45bfbfa admits only the hours 2026-10-19T08 to 2026-10-21T16 and exits `REFUSED path outside the plan` for every other file, so it cannot read the declared window (an error in QP's ruling, corrected by quant-proof comment 6329033726 on PR #790, which supersedes the ruling on this point). (It is a decision-time counter; it is not an r4b, l3 LightGBM or LAYA model, and uses no model features, so §3.1 is not touched. The ruling first read only the counter's header and trigger-test lines; per quant-proof comment 6329033726 on PR #790, quant-proof then read every line of `l3_count.py` at c45bfbfa and states that it reads decision-time fields, reads prints up to s0 + 420 s (TRACK_S) only for wallet buy counts and sums (the BOOST detector and the separate `boosted` count), computes no price, fill, exit, P&L or return, and makes no RPC call. That is a header, import and keyword check, not a line-by-line audit; the full-read statement is quant-proof's. The revised counter differs from `l3_count.py` in the one `PATH_RE` line only.)

H is 14 for any rate of at least 150/14 (about 10.714; the exact value governs) a day and is capped by the end-seal. The proxy's error cannot change either decision unless the true rate is below the floor.

A proxy count that merely clears 2.0 is NOT a pass. Every other cell is report-only. Below the bar the result is NOT_DECIDABLE, not a drop: no registration, no re-count, no other window. The only route left is then a live P0 from the Q50 process's own trigger records (3 full days after it starts, under EXP-024 Amendment 10 from its start), and the clock moves to 2026-10-28 or later.

Declared now, before any count. **2026-10-20 is excluded** on gap metadata alone, decided before any count: its 17:00 hour is an all-feed outage of 3,395 s (`COUNTS.md` quality line), a hard gap. No other window is tried after a failure.

The counter logs gaps but does not apply this rule; either a committed wrapper with a test applies it mechanically to the counter's JSON, or the manager applies it from the JSON under the fixed-first text. The rule is fixed before the job runs.
   6. **Job and notebook.** The job is submitted at or after 2026-10-24T00:10:00Z, after the fixed-first notebook entry (with its instant, both placeholders filled) is posted. A passing P0 sets `rate` = the mean of the 3 days; `H = min(90, max(14, ceil(150 / rate)))` (Amendment 4 item 2.2) is 14 at any rate of at least 150/14 (about 10.714; exact) a day.
   7. **Day 3 is a lower bound.** The counter reads prints for 300 s after a pool's s0 (TRACK_S 420 s for the BOOST test), but its file range ends at 2026-10-23T23. Pools with s0 near the end of 2026-10-23 whose first 300 s spill into 2026-10-24T00 are cut off, so day 3's count is a lower bound (the conservative direction for a pass bar of 'at least'). Day 1 and day 2 are not cut off in that way.

2. **The book.**
   1. **bot_id** `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1`, rule `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q50 v1`, `q_star_sol` 50. The id (i) starts with `h5v1`, so the scorer prices the 330 s exit; (ii) does not start with `h5v1-boostfloor-`, so the legacy `set_window_end` can never end it; (iii) starts with `h5`, so `a3_halt` requires it to be listed. Any other id would need the same three properties, shown in a test.

The books-file swap (a2 books plus Q50) happens in the same command as registration, away from :05 to :20 of 07, 13 and 19. The new file's sha256 and a verbatim copy go in the Q50 records PR and the notebook copy before the clock (Amendment 4 item 1.10). One full green a3-daily run on the new books file (all RCs 0, verdict NONE, six flags clear) is required before the clock.
   3. **Rent is CHARGED in the deciding cell**, with the rent-0 leg report-only, the same as the a2 siblings. Picking rent-0 for the sibling with the most trades would be a favourable-direction degree of freedom. Deciding cell: the 1.9 s leg at 0.30 SOL, the same as the a2 siblings.

The Q50 scoring wrapper `tools/arena_h5_score_q50.py` (a separate PR, QP G4) refuses any dir unless every start record has `q_star_sol == 50.0` and rule `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q50 v1`, and drops pv triggers whose `s0_t_recv_ms` is missing or below `clock_start_ms` + 60,000 (counted, not attempts). None of `tools/arena_h5_score.py`, `tools/arena_h5_score_a2.py`, `tools/arena_h5_window_count.py`, `tools/a3_redeploy_check.py` or `tools/a3_redeploy_pins.json` is edited while the a2 books run (Amendment 3 item 8(i)).

If any QP GO item slips, the clock moves to the next 00:00Z after the last item. Nothing is cut to keep a date. Window `[2026-10-25T00Z, 2026-11-06T00Z)`: 12 dates.
   2. **N + 1** from the clock (Amendment 4 item 2.5). It is disclosed on the a2 reads too; their multiplicity blocks are written at read time and list it. A screen pass of Q50 enters confirmation automatically (Amendment 3 item 2) and adds 1 to c.
   3. **Siblings and best-of-three in-sample (Amendment 3 item 8(e)).** `h5v1-boostfloor-a2`, `h5v2-boostclock-a2` and Q50 share the trigger family and the entry legs, and Q50's triggered pools contain every v1 pool. At the read, the best of the three is in-sample: a Q50 screen pass while v1-a2 fails is selection on a correlated sibling, not new evidence. Every read of any of the three prints all three side by side, with the shared-pool count (counts only).
   4. **Winner's curse.** Q50 is a discarded discovery cell (Q* = 40 best and monotone). The only edge figure cited for the family (+4.939% per attempt, best of 413 variants, enrichment p 0.1953) is Q40 and in-sample; no Q50 edge exists. Expect a lower per-trade edge than v1. At 1.0 SOL the cited impact (median 5.94%) exceeds the cited Q40 edge.
   5. **Kill-review week.** A kill-review week in `[registration, 2026-10-31]` stops the registration; the manager writes the check line in the registration notebook entry.
5. **A3 guard (h) cover (QP G6).**

With that proof, the A3 decoder parity run on the `1f7a89c` decoders transfers to Q50, because its decode functions are the same bytes. The proof is QP's G2 item (iv).
   3. **Fill at merge.** Q50 merge commit and proof: Q50 merge commit `a5f413704eb120c8f529101a6186e3a95de7f83e` (PR #789, head `10c574f`); proof `tools/test_h5_shadow_qstar_identity.py` at that commit (blob `cde0131a61117737219a84509190c40664f9565b`, sha256 `cefd13fa1d3b4a0dc00b51332233a3e6841fb4082ff13f745fe64b075be3a171`); `tools/h5_shadow.py` at that commit has sha256 `a5a1bdd7d30e21dd40d403826b216ec067e034db42d9e537b839057a6f79b5e0`. The Q50 book is not registered, and this cover is not claimed, until this placeholder is filled. It is filled ON THIS BRANCH (the PR that carries this amendment), before merge, once the Q50 flag PR (#789) has merged and its merge commit and proof exist; this amendment does not merge with the placeholder in it, and quant-proof re-checks the filled head (an OK given before the fill does not carry to it). This is the only placeholder in this amendment.
   4. **Not acceptable:** editing the pins or the check tool while the a2 books run (Amendment 3 item 8(i)).

QP OK and reviewer verdict: on the PR at its merged head; the records PR cites the comment ids. **Not measured by this amendment:** P0, any count, md5 or parity; the Q50 trigger rate on the shadow's own feed (the arena-tape counts are an upper bound on a different feed); fast-0 memory headroom; whether a kill-review week exists after 2026-10-25; the owner notebook entry `n_e4wMMd_5XtLT4A` (not opened by the writer).


### Amendment 6 (written 2026-10-22T19:20Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; revised 2026-10-22T19:24Z on quant-proof's comment 6349333799 on PR #797 (edits E1 to E8 and the minor item); before the clock of any book it covers and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)
DEC-031 Amendment 6: smoke-then-clock for new paper books. For a covered book, the 24 h pre-clock shakedown that gates a clock is replaced by (1) a short live smoke with a blocking list that concerns only whether the process runs, and (2) data-integrity checks computed per UTC hour inside the window, whose failing hours are excluded. Records only. No arena tape, ledger, `bots.json` line, outcome, fill, exit or P&L was opened to write it. Nothing here says any book makes money, and no book has cleared the promotion gate.
- Takes effect on merge, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict on the PR (the merge instant is the committer time of the squash-merge commit on origin/main, as in Amendment 2 item 2).
- **Scope.** Every book whose registration record (§1.2: its `bots.json` line and notebook copy) is after the merge. The rule is uniform and no book chooses between this regime and an older one. A running or already registered book, and the sheets, shakedown check files and rulings pinned for it, are unchanged and keep their own pass conditions. If this amendment merges after a book's registration, that book keeps the regime it registered under; there is no switch after the clock.
- **HV4.** HV4 is covered if and only if its registration record is after the merge. If HV4 registers before this amendment merges, it is not covered and Amendment 2 item 2's clean-24 h-stub condition stays for it. If it is covered, item 1.4 replaces Amendment 2 item 2's sentence that T0 is "the first 00:00Z after a clean 24 h stub run at the final sha and the registration merge". Amendment 2's other conditions for HV4 ((i) and (ii) and items 5 to 9) are unchanged. The HV4 stubs ran StubModel, so the timing of this choice carries no outcome information.
- **Order, as in Amendment 3 item 1 (the registration record comes before the clock).** An item of this amendment that a covered book's records PR pins is fixed before that book's clock.

## Why
HV4's clock slipped from 2026-10-21 to 10-22, 10-23 and 10-24. Each slip followed a NOT_PASSED operational check of a 24 h stub (scan-time p99, feed-sample coverage, a register defect, memory), and each required a fix, a new sha or tool pin and a fresh 24 h stub before any clock could start. None of those failures concerned the rule, a decision or an outcome. A paper book risks no money. What makes a paper read honest is the frozen sha, the pre-registered deciding cell and read instant, and exclusions that are decided without seeing outcomes. A day-long gate on process health adds none of those and costs a day or more per defect. This amendment keeps the three honesty anchors, moves the checks that protect data integrity from a gate before the clock to an outcome-blind filter inside the window, and turns performance and load figures into report-only numbers.

## Items
1. **Before the clock.** All of these hold, and the registration record states each with its evidence:
   1. **Declared and pinned, unchanged:** the `frozen_sha` (a commit on origin), the rule, the deciding cell and stake, and the read rule (the outcome-blind boundary instant of §2). Also unchanged as pre-clock requirements: §1.4's lamport parity on the committed parity set and its pins (parity reference, parity set, and the sha256 of `tools/arena_feed.py`, `tools/arena_paper.py` and `tools/arena_score.py`); Amendment 2's records-PR items for a covered book (the config pin of item 8, the V-band check of item 6 and Amendment 3 item 1); Amendment 3 item 1's P0 count and one-process-per-sha rule; and, for H5 books, Amendments 4 and 5 (the A3 books file, the P0 replacement, the Q50 stand-in and the guard (h) cover).
   2. **Unit tests** for the book's code are green at the `frozen_sha`; the records PR cites the run.
   3. **A live smoke of at least 2 h** on the real feed at the `frozen_sha`, from a clean checkout, in a scratch root that is not the book's root and is never counted. The smoke is an arena process under §3 and §4. Where §3.5 (H5 Look-2 seal) or §3.7 (C1-NF seal) covers the outcomes the smoke would create, it runs with pricing off (decision rows only) or it does not run. Its output is never an input to any read. It is judged only on this blocking list:
      - (a) the process starts and registers (its start row and registry call succeed);
      - (b) it writes decision rows (at least one row of the decision log, or for a book whose rule can be silent for 2 h, its scan or heartbeat rows, with the reason named in the registration record before the smoke starts);
      - (c) no crash and no traceback in its log, and no restart;
      - (d) memory: the fitted slope of resident set size over the smoke, times the planned window, plus the starting size, is at most the planned cap (the cap is pinned before the smoke starts; slope and size are read from the process's own RSS samples);
      - (e) where a port or re-implementation of an earlier tool exists, the md5 decision-equivalence proof of the lab's runner-change rule on a pinned replay, run before the clock;
      - (f) **for an LLM book:** the smoke calls the real model at the pinned model IDs and sampling settings, and calls succeed and parse, with API errors and parse failures at or below a number pinned before the smoke starts. A StubModel smoke does not meet item 1.3 for an LLM book. (A silent decision-changing fault is the class that health checks miss, and the md5 proof of (e) does not apply to model output.)

      A scan-time percentile, a feed-sample coverage figure, a chain-call rate or any other operational figure is NOT on the blocking list. It is computed and recorded from the smoke and blocks nothing. Counts, timings and flags only: the smoke's decision and ledger rows are not opened for price, fill, exit or P&L. The blocking list is written, with its instant, in a notebook entry before the smoke starts. A smoke FAIL means fix and re-smoke at the new sha, and the book gets no clock until a smoke passes at its final `frozen_sha`.
   4. **Clock.** The first 00:00Z after the smoke has passed at the book's final `frozen_sha` and the registration record is notebooked (Amendment 3 item 1: never earlier). The book then runs from a clean checkout at the `frozen_sha`, in its own root.
2. **Data integrity during the window.**
   1. **Tool and forbidden inputs.** A pinned, outcome-blind report tool, pinned as path@sha256 in the registration record before the clock. It reads only feed-status files, tape-hour file names and sizes, sample files, and the books' heartbeat and scan rows as liveness (present or absent), by timestamp. It is forbidden to read: ledgers, fills, prices, exits, strand or position state, open-position counts, P&L, decision-row contents, and any count of decisions or per-position calls used as a threshold. It prints no mint, wallet or signature.
   2. **What may exclude an hour (data integrity only).** An hour may be excluded only by a data-integrity check on an input that the frozen rule or the engine reads:
      - a feed or tape gap, a missing sample or tape-hour file, or a metric the tool cannot compute for the hour (a missing sample, an unreadable status file: such an hour fails, it does not pass, and later evidence does not replace it);
      - staleness of a decision input, defined computably: at each scan instant, the age (scan instant minus the receive time of the newest decision-input row, from the tape or pool-table timestamps) exceeds a per-book pinned threshold in seconds. No load metric enters it. The receive time must be stamped by the feed, tape or sampler process, never by the book's own process; the age of an input that only the book itself writes is report-only and never excludes an hour (QP 6349425527: the book's own lag grows with open positions, so with outcomes).

      **Report-only, never excluding:** scan or decision latency, exit-check delay, RSS or memory, call budgets, decision counts, and anything that is a function of the books' decisions, open positions, exits, or the calls made for them. They are computed per UTC hour and printed. Memory failures reach the read through crashes (item 4). (Reason: latency is already priced from the write instant by §1.4, so excluding slow hours would remove predictably worse trades, a bias in the book's favour; and load metrics follow open positions, which follow outcomes.)
   3. **Thresholds and labels.** Each check has a numeric threshold per UTC hour (unit and denominator stated), pinned in the registration record before the clock and never changed on a counted window. The records PR labels each threshold **price-path** (the feed the engine prices from) or **decision-input**.
   4. **Excluded hour.** A UTC hour that fails any pinned check is EXCLUDED for every covered book that reads that host or feed. The decision is automatic, from the tool's output alone. Whole UTC hours are used.
      - An attempt DECIDED in an excluded hour (either label) is excluded by decision instant. It neither counts (§2's 100 attempts on 5 UTC dates, the read boundary) nor is read, and it counts toward item 3's 10% cap only.
      - An attempt decided in a healthy hour whose decision-to-exit span overlaps a price-path excluded hour is excluded under §1.4's feed-gap rule and counts toward §1.4's 5% cap only.
      - No attempt counts in both caps.
   5. **Per-book pins only.** A book's excluded hours are computed only with the tool and thresholds pinned in its own registration record, over the feeds and host it names. A later book's pins never change an earlier book's excluded hours.
   6. **Daily table and changes.** The per-hour table and its output sha256 are written to a notebook entry at least once per UTC day, beside §1.2's daily decision-log sha256. A tool change (with §1.4's pin rule and quant-proof's OK; thresholds never change on a counted window, item 2.3) acts only on hours that start after the change is notebooked, and never re-decides an hour already tabled. A confirmation window (§2 step 4) uses the same pins as the screen.
   7. **Report.** Every read of a covered book prints the per-hour table (counts only), the excluded hours with the failing check and its label, the report-only figures, the attempts excluded by each route, and the attempts kept per UTC date.
3. **Cap.** If more than 10% of a book's attempts are decided in excluded hours, the read is NOT_DECIDABLE, final for the `bot_id` (§2). The denominator is all attempts decided before the read boundary, counted by decision timestamp only, with §2's count tool. One denominator: there is no cap on hours. §1.4's 5% cap (trades excluded by the feed-gap rule, including the span exclusions of item 2.4 and the restart exclusions of item 4) stays separate, on its own denominator, and so do Amendment 1's and Amendment 4 item 1.13's. The first cap to trigger decides, and none widens, resets or replaces another. Expected: for short-hold books the 10% cap binds first, and for long-hold books §1.4's 5% does.
4. **Crash and restart.** A crash or restart of a covered book's process, restarted from a clean checkout at the same `frozen_sha`, creates no new clock and no new `bot_id`. The restart gap (from the last decision or heartbeat row before the stop to the first row after the restart) is excluded in whole UTC hours, rounded outward, as decision-input hours, and recorded in a notebook entry with the instants. For a book covered by this amendment, this item governs: positions open at a stop are §1.4 exclusions (process_restart / engine_restart) counted in its 5% cap. Amendment 3 item 8(j) is unchanged for every other book. A restart on any other sha is a new `bot_id` with a new clock (§1.2). An open attempt left unclosed for more than 24 h after a restart is an orphan and makes the read NOT_DECIDABLE (DEC-014 Amendment 1; §2), as now.
5. **What does not change.** The promotion gate and its thresholds; §2's deciding cell, its one read per `bot_id`, its outcome-blind read instant, the screen and confirmation steps and the multiple-testing c of Amendment 3 item 2 (an excluded hour changes the attempt count, never c); the multiplicity block of Amendment 3 item 3 (N counts from the clock; smoke runs are not books and do not count in N); the seals and §3; §1.4 (parity, pins, costs, fills, the 5% cap); Amendment 2's rent and its conditions; Amendment 4's A3 rule and Amendment 5's Q50 path for H5 books; the ban on a live send (§4); and every clock and pinned sheet of a running or registered book.

## Risks
- **Exclusion selection bias.** An excluded hour is a degrees-of-freedom surface if the exclusion can depend on outcomes. The design closes that this way: only data-integrity checks may exclude (item 2.2); load and performance figures, which follow open positions and so follow outcomes, are report-only; the tool is forbidden every outcome-linked input (item 2.1); thresholds, labels and the tool's sha256 are pinned in the book's own record before the clock (items 2.3, 2.5); the rule is automatic; the table is notebooked daily (item 2.6). Under that design an exclusion is a function of feed and host integrity in that hour, the same for every covered book that reads it, and cannot select good hours for a particular book.
- **Residual bias that remains.** Feed gaps cluster in busy, volatile hours, so excluded hours skew toward high-activity periods, and the sign of the effect on P&L is unknown. §1.4's feed-gap exclusion has the same property (Amendment 1 bias note). Failing hours and attempts both cluster in busy hours, so the cap is stated on attempts (item 3), where it bounds the lost share directly. It bounds the size of the effect, not its direction.
- **A smoke is weaker than a 24 h stub.** A 2 h smoke can miss a fault with a daily period (a day-boundary rollover, a nightly job, memory growth after 2 h, a stale cache). Those are found inside the window, and cost part of it through crashes (item 4) or integrity exclusions (item 2). A fault that silently changes decisions is not covered by health checks at all. The unit tests, the frozen sha, the md5 proof (item 1.3(e)) and the real-model smoke for LLM books (item 1.3(f)) are the guards for that class.
- **Smoke outcomes under a seal.** A smoke on the live feed creates paper outcomes. Item 1.3 applies §3 and §4 to it, with pricing off or no run where §3.5 or §3.7 covers them.
- **Date pressure.** The smoke removes a delay spent on health, not on the rule. Nothing in this amendment lowers the gate, and nothing is cut to keep a date: a failed blocking-list item still means no clock.

**Not measured by this amendment:** any threshold value, any smoke, any hour's health, any count or any outcome. The report tool, its tests and the thresholds are owed in each covered book's records PR before its clock. QP OK and reviewer verdict: on the PR at its merged head.

### Amendment 7 (written 2026-10-22T19:43:58Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before the clock of any book it covers and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)

1. **What changes.** Amendment 4 item 2.2 says H "is pinned in the `bots.json` line before the clock". For a book whose registration code at its frozen sha cannot write H into its `bots.json` line, H is pinned instead in BOTH (a) the notebook copy of its `bots.json` lines (§1.2; the same notebook entry that carries the copy) and (b) the book's records PR (for HV4, PR #779 or its successor), as a commit pushed to origin before the clock whose sha is cited in the notebook entry of (a). Both places must give the same H. A missing copy, or two copies that differ, means no clock. For HV4, H = 14 days by Amendment 4 item 2.2's window-end cap (QP ruling P3). The `bots.json` line itself carries no H; that is the only difference from Amendment 4 item 2.2. Every other word of Amendment 4 item 2.2 applies unchanged: H is computed from the P0 rate by the same formula, capped by any end-seal or window end, and never extended.
2. **Which books.** HV4 at frozen sha 9ed4c5ff63935696b2a89150e13565e123047754: `register_bot` (tools/arena_paper.py lines 265-277 at that sha) writes a fixed field set with no H field, and tools/arena_paper.py is a §1.4 pin, so adding the field would need a new sha and a new parity proof. No other book is covered: a later book in the same position needs its own amendment with a QP OK and a reviewer verdict. H5-Q50 (`h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1`) is not covered: it keeps Amendment 5 item 4 (notebook copy and the records PR).

Only the place where H is written changes, and both places are fixed before the clock and visible to every reader.
4. **What does not change.** The promotion gate, DEC-031 §2 screen and confirmation steps, c, N, the seals, and Amendments 1 to 6 except the one place in Amendment 4 item 2.2 that item 1 names.
5. **Order.** This amendment must merge before HV4's registration (HV4-EXEC-1022.md Step 3).

### Amendment 8 (written 2026-10-23T10:20Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records plus the code of PR `claude/arena-send-160k`; before the clock of any book it covers and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)
DEC-031 Amendment 8: the deciding send cost for NEW arena books is 160,000 lamports (155,000 priority + 5,000 base), not 505,000. This turns DEC-019 Amendment 2's fee-arm PASS (`ARTIFACTS/lab/feearm-landing-2026-10-23.md`) and quant-proof's ruling (`ARTIFACTS/lab/qp-ruling-1023-feearm.md`, item 9: "DEC-031 for new arena books: YES, an amendment is required") into DEC-031 text. It is a cost input, not a P&L result. No book changes status, and no recomputed history counts as out-of-sample. No book has cleared the promotion gate. No arena tape, ledger, `bots.json` line, outcome, fill, exit or P&L was opened to write it.
- Takes effect on merge, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict on the PR (the merge instant is the committer time of the squash-merge commit on origin/main, as in Amendment 2 item 2). It must merge before the first clock it covers.

**Items**
1. **What changes (§1.4 "Costs and fills", the 505,000 per send).** For a covered book the deciding send cost is **160,000 lamports per send**, the same in the buy, the sell, the guarded-buy single send, the stranded leg, the dust-sell rule (proceeds below one send) and the fail blend (flat 15% and the pressure rule). The lag shift added to the simulator is **0 slots** (QP item 6: the measured shift was -0.238 slots, driven by three control outliers; the probe tested "not slower", not "faster", so no latency credit is taken; crediting one needs its own pre-registered test). The pinned wording of the DEC-019 Amendment 2 verdict is: "At 155,000 priority, 150/152 sends landed vs 146/148 at 500,000 (lower bound -2.70 pp, margin -5); buy lag 2.20 vs 2.44 slots (upper bound -0.007, margin +0.5)."
2. **Which books are covered.** A book is covered if and only if (a) its registration record (the `bots.json` line) is after this amendment's merge, (b) its `frozen_sha` contains the Amendment 8 code, and (c) its executor configuration pins **buy AND sell priority at exactly 155,000 lamports**, with every retry or escalation at or below 155,000 or priced at its own value in the stack; the records PR cites that configuration as `path:line@sha` and a test that pins it. An executor at 55,000 (H5), 500,000 or any other value keeps 505,000: 160,000 is honest only for a path that really sends at 155,000. **Today no executor qualifies:** `h5_executor.py:267-268` sets 55,000 / 150,000, `arena_live_bridge.py:854` sets 500,000 and `probe_executor.py` sends at 500,000. So **no book is covered until one does**; this amendment adds the stack and the guards, it does not add an executor and does not authorise a live send (§4 stands). A 155,000-priority executor needs its own reviewed change.
3. **Never retroactive.** No registered or running book is covered: HV4 (frozen sha 9ed4c5f), H5 v1/v2 a2, H5-Q50, the books run by `tools/arena_rules_v2.py` (FLOOR) and every other book keep 505,000 for their whole counted window, with their pinned stacks and sheets unchanged. A mid-window repricing is a changed stack, and a book that reprices is a new book. Reruns of old screens at 160,000 are exploration only: in-sample, never out-of-sample, never a gate input; a cell that turns positive only because the fee fell is a new hypothesis and subject to winner's curse across every cell rerun.
4. **The code (this PR).** The pinned host `tools/arena_rules.py` is **byte-identical to main** (not edited, not re-pinned). A new module `tools/arena_stack_v2.py` holds `STACK_FIELDS_V2`, `stack_config_v2()` (identical to `stack_config()` in every field except `send = 160_000`), `stack_config_for(version, d)` (1 or 2, anything else refused) and `check_ledger_send`. `tools/arena_rules_v2.py` gains `--stack-version {1,2}` with **default 1**; `tools/arena_screen.py:screen_cfg` gains `send` (default 505,000); `tools/arena_stack_v2.py` also holds the read wrapper `report_main` (`python -m tools.arena_stack_v2 --expect-send 160000 <arena_report_legs args>`); `tools/arena_report_legs.py` (a pin of `arena_screen.REPORT_LEGS_PIN`) is not edited. `tools/arena_paper.py` (the §1.4 sha pin, sha256 `9d2fdb04a3da9bb38de9e30c91154f5a9ab2de513e6f4d7936c1eb1a54863514`), `tools/arena_stack.py`, `tools/arena_h5_cost.py` and `scripts/research/arena-rules-job.sh` / `arena-rules-v2-job.sh` are not edited. A covered book runs through the new `scripts/research/arena-rules-v2-send-job.sh`, which requires `ARENA_RULES_STACK_VERSION` (no default) and passes `--stack-version`; the pinned job scripts do not pass it, so they price 505,000. **Resume and read safety:** `HostV2.resume` refuses to continue a ledger with any row whose `stack.send` differs from the process's send, and the read wrapper refuses (exit 3) any row at another send than the book's pin (`--expect-send`, required in a covered book's read command, which runs `arena_report_legs` through the wrapper). **The records PR pins the exact command line (job script env, `--stack-version 2`, `--feed-delay-slots`) and `stack_config_v2(d).as_dict()`** next to the config pin of Amendment 2 item 8.
5. **Parity proof (§1.4's lamport parity, for the new stack).** `tools/test_arena_send_160k.py` on the committed parity set (`tools/fixtures/arena_paper/parity_cases.jsonl`, sha256 `8b4d309b833da29200c7ebece3d4e0d4cc352f47def94560223b6c86026a4f58`): (a) the default configuration reproduces the committed references lamport-exact and the existing md5 pins pass unchanged; (b) the 160,000 stack differs from the 505,000 stack by exactly 2 x 345,000 lamports on every filled round trip, exactly 345,000 on a stranded leg and on a guarded buy (one send), the E1 floor and the pressure p are unchanged, and the flat and pressure legs move by the formula; (c) the pins for the new configuration only. **One state change is real and is declared:** the dust rule (rule `total`: a sell whose proceeds are below one send is a strand) moves with the send, so a sell worth 160,000 to 505,000 lamports is a strand at 505,000 and a tiny fill at 160,000. On the committed set that is 12 (case, stake, latency) legs of the rugged pool `ps048`; rule `sc` has none. These are not "differs by 2 x 345,000" cases and the test counts them separately.
6. **Printed comparison.** Every read of a covered book also prints the 505,000 leg for comparison only (QP). The deciding legs are the 160,000 legs.
7. **The dust-rule flip is disclosed in every read.** Each of the 12 `ps048` flips moves its leg by 2,384,280 to 2,729,280 lamports (the strand's -stake -send -2,039,280 rent against a tiny fill), which is 7 to 8 times the 690,000 round-trip saving, and it moves **in favour of (is optimistic for)** the covered stack when a rug sells for 160,000 to 505,000 (a strand becomes a fill). Each read of a covered book prints the **count of flipped legs and their SOL**, and the promotion gate must still pass with those legs priced as strands (the 505,000 dust threshold). A book that passes only with the flipped legs priced as fills does not pass. The read code that prints the flip count and SOL and re-checks the gate with flipped legs priced as strands must be merged, with a test, before the first covered book's clock (QP 6363162815).

**What does not change**
The promotion gate and its thresholds; §2's deciding cell, one read per `bot_id`, the screen and confirmation steps; the multiple-testing c and N (a covered book counts in N from its clock; the cheaper send adds no count of its own); the seals and §3; §1.4's parity requirement, its other pins and the 5% cap; Amendment 2's rent rules (filled-trip rent 0, stranded 2,039,280); the latency legs (1.3 s and 1.9 s) and the exit lag; the E1 floor (384,022 per 0.05 SOL, DEC-021); the haircuts; the 15% flat fail and the pressure model; the ban on a live send (§4); every executor's own priority (H5 55,000 / 150,000, the live bridge and probe 500,000) until changed deliberately; and every clock and pinned sheet of a running or registered book.

**Risks**
- **The measurement is thin at peak congestion.** Only 24 of 150 probe buys fell in 10Z to 18Z (16%); the busiest hours are thinly sampled. A 160,000 stack may be optimistic in the hours when congestion is highest. Residual risk, direction unknown, stated here as QP asked.
- **Sell-side watch item.** Test-arm sells landed 75/77 vs 73/73 at 500,000; both failures landed on chain with `slippage_exceeded` (descriptive split: lower bound -7.55 pp, Fisher p 0.497). Any live 155,000 book tracks its sell slippage-fail share against the 505,000 history.
- **Test A was fragile:** three more failed test-arm sends would have moved its lower bound below -5 pp (QP ruling, section 1). C1 and C2 closed the logging gap (lab note).
- **A cheaper send helps small-edge books most, and it is a per-trade cost cut, not an edge.** It does not make any book positive and the gate is unchanged.

### Amendment 9 (written 2026-10-23T17:11:05Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before the clock of the book it covers, before its P0 count and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)
DEC-031 Amendment 9: the read horizon of FLOOR (`l1-floor-v2`, the 24 h-hold PumpSwap line, pack `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt24/floor-1026/`) is pinned at **H = 12 days**, in place of Amendment 4 item 2's floor of 14, so that the runner, the clock and the 24 h drain fit in one MiScusi job under the platform's hard 14-day maximum with no in-clock handover. Basis: quant-proof's fast-track ruling `hunt24/floor-1026/QP-RULING-1023-floor-fasttrack.md` (sha256 5324194b…) item 1, and its ADDENDUM 1 (`QP-RULING-1023-floor-fasttrack-ADDENDUM1.md`). No outcome, fill, exit or P&L of any forward book was opened to write it. No book has cleared the promotion gate; FLOOR's figures are in-sample only.
1. **Why it cannot create a pass.** H only sets the instant at which an unread book ends NOT_DECIDABLE. The read stays at the first boundary with at least 100 attempts on at least 5 dates (§2, Amendment 4 item 2); the gate and its thresholds are unchanged.
2. **Pre-declared rate condition.** FLOOR registers under this amendment only if `rate_att_lo` (not `H_days_att_lo`, which applies the 14-day floor), printed by the pinned FLOOR P0 tool `tools/floor_p0_count.py` at the merge commit of PR #810, `9a403a39b6450b5fcd60c5816867ce2ec75ed403` (file sha256 `e6186ac737d7ca7fcf6be7d369ca25f96b0744ceb18eca3648099546204705b9`), over **exactly 3 distinct OK days within {2026-10-22, 2026-10-23, 2026-10-24}**, is **at least 12.5 attempts per day** (150 / 12). This amendment merges before that count runs. **Below 12.5, or fewer than 3 distinct OK days: no registration on this window, and the multiplicity counter N is not spent.** Amendment 4 does not apply as a fallback (it would need an in-clock handover).
3. **H pin (FLOOR's own; Amendment 7 covers HV4 only).** H = 12 days from the clock instant. END_H = clock + 12 d. No attempt decided at or after END_H counts. If the read boundary (100 attempts on 5 dates) is not reached before END_H, the book is NOT_DECIDABLE, final; N increases by 1; no extension and no handover. The H pin is recorded in the notebook copy and the records PR, as Amendment 7 does for HV4.
4. **Smoke and job.** Amendment 6 item 3 applies: the smoke runs in its own scratch root and job. The runner job spans at most from clock − 22 h to END_H + 25 h (13 d 23 h). Its time limit is set to at least (END_H + 25 h − its start), at most the 14-day cap, checked against the job's submit time before submission; a limit that cannot cover END_H + 25 h means no registration on this window.
5. **Gap precondition (replaces C11 of `QP-RULING-1023-floor.md` for this book).** Over the 72 closed hours ending no later than 2 h before registration (E3), the tape must show **0 `data_loss` rows of any gap kind (slot_jump, socket_reconnect, process_restart or other) whose program is PumpSwap, `all`, or missing or unknown**, counted from the feed's own gap rows with **no reclassification**. pump.fun-only rows are not on FLOOR's price path (FLOOR trades PumpSwap pools only; the smoke window of 2026-10-22T21..10-23T03 alone had 323 of them); they are not filtered anywhere, and whether they affect FLOOR is decided only by item 6's pinned, unfiltered dry run. The single exception is the gap rows written by the one planned feed hand-off of item 7 (its `process_restart` row and the PumpSwap `feed_restart` row it also writes), pinned by the MiScusi job ids of the cancelled and the new feed job, spanning at most 600 s in total.
6. **Exclusion dry run.** Before registration, `arena_hour_health` at its pinned sha with FLOOR's thresholds file pinned before the run (both shas in the records PR) is run over the same 72 h; the share of 24 h spans touching a price-path-failed hour must be **at most 5%**. No program filter or other change is applied for this dry run.
7. **Feed hand-off before the clock.** The arena feed hand-off (same sha and command, the new 14-day job) happens before the clock and inside the item 5 window as its single pinned restart, per quant-proof's feed hand-off ruling `hunt28/l2/QP-RULING-1023-feed-handover.md` (sha256 `170bd3bbf11ce8d0bcf9f59bd25895338c4d7d5e9331fd213ed271ad20a9bf62`): window 2026-10-24 12:10–12:25Z, after the HV4 A1 and X-rule part 2 steps and before D1/E4. No feed restart is planned inside the clock.
8. **One slip.** The pre-declared clock is 2026-10-26T00:00:00Z. If any of items 2, 4, 5, 6 or 7 is not met by 2026-10-25T22:00Z, the clock moves once to 2026-10-27T00:00:00Z, with every item re-checked against that instant. If they are not met then, FLOOR is not registered on this window and N is not spent. No further re-checks under this amendment.
9. **Unchanged.** Amendment 6 (caps, exclusions, NOT_DECIDABLE rules) and Amendment 8 (160,000 per send) apply. The 22% block-edge exclusion share of the in-sample screen is disclosed in the records PR; it does not block the clock.

### Amendment 9, Erratum 1 (written 2026-10-23T20:03Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before FLOOR's P0 count (A1), before G1, DR1 and E3, and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)

DEC-031 Amendment 9, Erratum 1: Amendment 9 items 8 and 9 are replaced as below, and the window of items 5 and 6 is the exact window of the new item 8. Basis: quant-proof's ruling `hunt24/floor-1026/QP-RULING-1023-am9-erratum.md` (sha256 `a914d1ff27b82e8dc20a2b473429a86c3dc3be1f6792e880001cf869a04f22b3`), items D-A (conditions E1 to E7) and D-B. FLOOR has 0 out-of-sample trades and has not cleared the promotion gate. Every FLOOR figure is in-sample. No outcome, fill, exit, P&L or attempt count of any forward book was opened to write it. This erratum must merge before FLOOR's A1 job is submitted (2026-10-25T01:10Z); if it merges later, it does not apply, the 10-26 clock is not used, and the merged text's single slip to 2026-10-27T00:00:00Z (22:00Z deadline) is the only path.

1. **Item 8, replaced in full:**
   "8. **One slip.** The pre-declared clock is 2026-10-26T00:00:00Z. The deadline is CLOCK - 15 min (2026-10-25T23:45:00Z). Registration (E3) happens before the deadline, and items 2, 4, 5, 6 and 7 are met before E3. The window of items 5 and 6 is exactly the 72 closed hours [CLOCK - 75 h, CLOCK - 3 h) = [2026-10-22T21:00:00Z, 2026-10-25T21:00:00Z) (G1/DR1 END_ISO 2026-10-25T21:00:00Z, window_to_ms 1792962000000), so E3 lies in [2026-10-25T23:00:00Z, 2026-10-25T23:45:00Z). No other window is used. If any of items 2, 4, 5, 6 or 7 is not met before the deadline, the clock moves once to 2026-10-27T00:00:00Z, with every item re-checked by the same formulas: deadline 2026-10-26T23:45:00Z, window [2026-10-23T21:00:00Z, 2026-10-26T21:00:00Z) (window_to_ms 1793048400000). If they are not met then, FLOOR is not registered on this window and N is not spent. No further re-checks under this amendment. Registration is final: once E3 has written the registry line, no slip applies."
2. **Items 5 and 6, window.** "The 72 closed hours ending no later than 2 h before registration (E3)" in item 5, and "the same 72 h" in item 6, mean the exact window of item 8 above, and nothing else. Item 5's hand-off exception (at most 600 s in total) is checked on the union of the exempt rows: max(to_ms) - min(from_ms) over G1's `exempt_handoff_rows` is at most 600,000 ms, and that figure is written in the notebook before E3.
3. **Item 9, the Amendment 8 clause, replaced:**
   "Amendment 8 applies on its own terms: FLOOR is not a covered book (Amendment 8 item 2(b) and (c); item 3 names it), so FLOOR is priced at 505,000 lamports per send in every leg (buy, sell, guarded buy, stranded leg, dust rule, flat 15% and pressure fail models) for its whole counted window, at frozen sha 5e12f8f5 (tools/arena_paper.py:84). Amendment 8 items 6 and 7 do not apply to FLOOR. The 160,000 figures in L3's reprice (hunt28/l3/REPRICE.md row 1) are exploration only, in-sample, never a gate input."
   The records PR quotes FLOOR's in-sample basis at 505,000 (archived flat mean +6.17% at 0.25 SOL, about +0.0154 SOL per trade). Any 160,000 figure appears only labelled "exploration at 160,000". Amendment 6 still applies as item 9 says.
4. **Item 6, DR1 thresholds file, pinned.** FLOOR's pre-clock dry-run thresholds file is exactly the bytes `{"max_gap_s":{"value":3600,"label":"price-path"}}` with no trailing newline (sha256 `41758769444a859dcdb24734cb7c86c93cb91f4dd1226de97ee74baa9928446f`; `hunt24/floor-1026/FLOOR-THRESHOLDS-DR1.json`), the same bytes as the A1 P0 gap file. `max_data_loss_count` is not pinned under any label, because it counts pump.fun spans, which FLOOR does not price from. This dry run is close to vacuous: it catches tape-level failures and gaps of 1 h or more, nothing finer. The fine-grained guards are item 5 (0 rows) and §1.4's per-trade gap rule in the window. 3600 is the A1/HV4 precedent value; the builder's preview dry run on an earlier window (0/48) is disclosed and was not a decision input. In the in-window thresholds file, the price-path checks are exactly this one entry, byte-identical in key, value and label; every sampler-based check is labelled decision-input. That file and X (the pack item 2 rule) are pinned in the records PR before E3, or Amendment 6 item 2.3 is not met and there is no clock.

Before E3 the manager writes in the notebook that G1's `window_to_ms` is 1792962000000 (slip: 1793048400000); any other value means item 5 is not met. No gap rows or hour-health output for any hour of either window are looked at before G1 runs; not running G1 by the deadline means item 5 is not met. **Arithmetic correction to the ruling.** Quant-proof's ruling text wrote the deadline as "CLOCK - 24 h 15 min" and the scripts' lead as 87300 s; CLOCK - 24 h 15 min is 2026-10-24T23:45Z, a day early. The instant 2026-10-25T23:45:00Z, which every other line of the ruling and this erratum uses (window end 10-25T21:00Z, E3 in [23:00Z, 23:45Z)), is CLOCK - 15 min. Likewise the merged item 8's 22:00Z is CLOCK - 2 h, not CLOCK - 26 h as EXEC-AM9-1026.md and its scripts stated. ### Amendment 10 (written 2026-10-24T00:49Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0 (commit time 00:49:42Z); records plus the code of PR #840 (`claude/a3-synthetic-pooled`); before the clock of any book it covers and before any arena read it touches; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)
DEC-031 Amendment 10: the A3 guard's `synthetic_share_high` flag is replaced, for the books named in item 2, by a pooled test. Basis: quant-proof's ruling `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt29/a3fix/QP-RULING-1024-a3-synthetic.md` (file time 2026-10-24T00:48:12Z; items 1 to 3 and the PR #840 conditions C1 to C5), and the finding in MiScusi notebook entry n_5ob4j6rvho4xuQ: the A3 guard run of 2026-10-22T19:10:05Z halted on `synthetic_share_high` at 8/19 = 0.421 and ended `h5v1-boostfloor-a2` and `h5v2-boostclock-a2` at `window_end_ms` 1792696205000, before their clock of 2026-10-23T00:00:00Z. The only monitor fields behind the ruling are `run_utc`, `graduations.synthetic.{n_checked,n_synthetic,share}`, the slot and time range of each sample and the flag. No book has cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here is a profit claim.
- Takes effect on merge, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head of PR #840 and a reviewer verdict on the PR (the merge instant is the committer time of the squash-merge commit on origin/main, as in Amendment 2 item 2). It must merge before 2026-10-25T00:00:00Z to cover Q50 (item 2). Code: PR #840 head `512f5225a8698949b89f285ca8caa3e11107257d` (to be merged to main as-is); main merge commit `79a93e94464e26d3f7246e637fac0ae58430e043`. **Guard pin:** branch `claude/a3-guard-pin-1024`, tag `a3-guard-pin-1024`, commit `0ec7fae89abe00021871f35258a8bc659b3df6bd` = 136cd98 plus exactly `tools/a3_redeploy_check.py` and `tools/test_a3_redeploy_check.py` from 512f5225. Check tool blob `4cf7e363b2bf9901e11310d101431b31202a43c3`, sha256 `1f84072da673cc451f9e3a6baa8b25d6295b497305ffe7bc38fef42e3a710f70`; `tools/h5_shadow.py` at the pin is blob `23b9ccc2562f4cb83113a8b9faa4cb8d9e86907f`.

**Items**
1. **The replacement rule (replaces the sentence "`synthetic_share_high` stays as pinned (above 0.35 is a flag ...)" of Amendment 4 item 1.2, for the books of item 2).** The constants are verbatim:
   - Pool the newest **K = 4** monitor records with `run_utc` at or before this run. A record whose `[slot_min, slot_max]` overlaps a newer included record is skipped (overlap removal by slot range, not by `run_utc`), so graduations are never counted twice.
   - Baseline pinned **p0 = 75/339** (the 18 post-redeploy runs, after 2026-10-08T16:20Z: 16 on research-0 and 2 on fast-0). Dispersion pinned **phi = 2.0** (observed 1.70, rounded up and declared).
   - **Halt iff z = (x - n*p0) / sqrt(phi * n * p0 * (1 - p0)) > 3.0902** (one-sided alpha 0.001 per evaluation), x and n the pooled synthetic and checked graduation counts.
   - If the newest record has no valid synthetic counts, or `n_checked` < 10, synthetic is **unevaluated** for this run (the pooled test does not borrow an older record's verdict). Earlier records in the window without valid counts are skipped from the pool. The existing rule for an unevaluated flag on two consecutive runs then makes it a hard halt.
   - Check (a) of Amendment 4 item 1.4 (the program-only redeploy check) reads the pooled synthetic decision, not the record's single-run flag. The single-run share is printed as a WARN and never decides.
   - The constants live in the check tool's blob with a pinned-constant test.
2. **Figures from the ruling (structure counts only).** The old rule halts when x/n > 0.35 on one run of 17 to 20. At the pooled base rate 0.2212 its per-run false-trip rate is about 0.10 (beta-binomial fitted to the observed overdispersion, chi2 28.8 on 17 df, phi 1.70: 0.157 per run, matching the observed 3 of 18 runs above 0.35), and P(at least one trip in 24 runs, a 12-day window at two runs a day) is about 0.98. The new rule, simulated under the beta-binomial null (20,000 paths): false trip 0.0014 per run and 0.023 over 24 runs. Detection: a sustained share of 0.45 for 2 days trips with probability 0.62, 0.60 for 2 days 0.99, a sustained 0.45 over the window 1.00. A raw 0.35 threshold on the pooled roughly 76 graduations would still false-trip 0.027 per evaluation and 0.33 over 24 runs (ruling C2), which is why the z-test is pinned instead. These are simulated, not observed on new data. What the flag guards after Amendment 4's per-pool exclusion of synthetic and unclassified pools is a regime alarm (a sustained shift in how graduations happen), not a per-trade protection.
3. **Scope.** The rule applies to the A3 guard for the H5 arena books listed in `new_rule_bot_ids` of `a3-books.json` (QP ruling scope), from the merge onward, for books whose clock is after the merge. The code decides for every listed book; a1 and a2 are unaffected only because their window ends are earlier and an end never moves later. It never applies to EXP-024, DEC-024 or DEC-026 (each has its own record-only or alert-only handling). **Q50** (`h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1`, registered 2026-10-24T00:20:15Z, clock 2026-10-25T00:00:00Z) is covered only if this amendment and the PR #840 code merge and the guard is redeployed (item 5) with the first official run green before 2026-10-25T00:00:00Z. If not, Q50 runs under the old flag for its whole life with no switch after the clock. **HV4 and FLOOR do not use the guard**: their ids are not in `a3-books.json`, are not h5-prefixed, and no code of theirs imports `a3_redeploy_check`, `a3_halt` or `a3-daily` (QP ruling 3.1, grep on `tools/` and `scripts/`). This amendment touches nothing they run. `tools/r4b_read.py` (EXP-027), which reads research-0's structure halts under EXP-027's own rule, is unchanged and out of scope. Am.3 item 8(i) (no edit while the a2 books run) no longer binds, because the a2 windows are closed and will never be read; the a2 shadow is stopped before the guard edit lands.
4. **End state of the a2 books (QP ruling item 1).** `h5v1-boostfloor-a2` and `h5v2-boostclock-a2` END **NOT_DECIDABLE**: window empty, 0 counted attempts, `window_end_ms` 1792696205000, final for their bot_ids. This is not VOID (VOID is for a missed GO step); it is a rule-driven end under Amendment 4 item 1.2 as registered, with no manual override (item 1.6). Each counts in N (+2, Amendment 3 item 3: every book with a clock before the read, stopped and NOT_DECIDABLE books included), noted as having had 0 counted attempts and no chance to pass. Record lines:
   - `H5_ARENA_END: h5v1-boostfloor-a2 NOT_DECIDABLE (A3 synthetic_share_high 8/19 = 0.421, run 2026-10-22T19:10:05Z, BEFORE clock 2026-10-23T00:00:00Z; counted window empty; 0 counted attempts) window_end_ms 1792696205000; FINAL`
   - `H5_ARENA_END: h5v2-boostclock-a2 NOT_DECIDABLE (same) window_end_ms 1792696205000; FINAL`
   - Scorer counts are added as printed in the records PR (triggers_seen, skipped_after_window, per date); no outcome figure is ever added. The a2 ids stay listed in `a3-books.json`.
   - **Revival** is allowed only as NEW bot_ids (for example `h5v1-boostfloor-a3`, `h5v2-boostclock-a3`) at the same frozen sha and rule, with a new clock, adding to N and disclosing the a2 end (Amendment 4 item 2.3), and only after this amendment and the fixed guard are merged and redeployed. Not under the old flag (P(trip within 24 runs) about 0.98). The minimum pack is the ruling's item 1.5 (a to g) and is not restated here.
5. **Redeploy order (QP ruling 3.4).** Not within :04 to :25 of 07, 13 or 19 UTC, and not in the 12:10 to 12:25Z feed hand-off; MemoryCurrent checked first. (1) Cancel the QUEUED successor #1332 (`after: 1318`) first and confirm it is cancelled; otherwise cancelling #1318 releases #1332 at the old pin. (2) Cancel #1318, then check with ps/pgrep that no `a3-daily`, `pump_structure_monitor` or `a3_redeploy_check` process is left. (3) Submit the new loop (`a3-loop.sh` with PIN = the guard pin above, **never main or the merge commit**: main's `tools/h5_shadow.py` is blob `f23cf492` since #789 and fails `frozen_guard`, so every pump or PumpSwap redeploy would ERROR and end Q50. The loop header checks the check-tool sha256 above and that `tools/h5_shadow.py` is blob `23b9ccc2` (QP D4). If the pin is not tested green on fast-0 by 06:30Z, #1318 stays at 136cd98 through the 07:10Z run and the redeploy happens before 19:10Z (QP D3); the same `a3-daily.sh` sha check, plus a sha256 check of `tools/a3_redeploy_check.py`; frozen-object checks for 1f7a89c and Q50's a5f41370 kept; resumable, 13 days, then a new successor at the new pin). (4) The new loop's immediate first run is an **official** A3 run and can halt; it must be green (all return codes 0, verdict NONE, six flags evaluated) before Q50's clock and replaces G9b as Q50's pre-clock green run. (5) Dead-man jobs #1319 and #1333 may stay at 136cd98 only if `a3_halt.py` and `a3-daily.sh` are byte-identical there. (6) Notebook entry with job ids, the new pin and sha256s; Q50's records PR is updated with them and this amendment before the clock.
6. **No shifting of guard runs.** No guard run is moved, skipped or delayed to avoid the flag; that would choose the measurement instant with the share in view. If the 07:10Z or 19:10Z run of 2026-10-24 trips the OLD flag before the fix lands, Q50's window ends before its clock: `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` is NOT_DECIDABLE (empty window), final, N +1, counts only; revival only as a new id keeping the Amendment 5 item 2.1 properties (starts `h5v1`, not `h5v1-boostfloor-`, starts `h5`), with the Q50 wrapper (`Q50_ID` is hard-coded) changed by its own PR, the P0 proxy reuse disclosed, a new shadow after registration, a books-file swap, a green run at the new pin and a records PR, and the clock the next 00:00Z after the last item. Nothing is cut to keep a date.
7. **Tests and proofs required on PR #840 before merge (QP conditions C1 to C5).** C1: check (a) uses the pooled decision (raw flag true with pooled clear: (a) does not fail; pooled over: HARD). C2: constants as in item 1 with a pinned-constant test. C3: slot-range overlap removal, tested with two overlapping records. C4: unevaluated when the newest record lacks valid counts or `n_checked` < 10, tested. C5: a replay over both daily files (research-0 and fast-0's own), every line, old flag, new z and new decision, counts only, posted on the PR; 0 changes on the other five flags and on pins (other verdict reasons byte-identical); tests: on main 167 pass and 1 fails (`test_frozen_guard_matches_the_shipped_pins_at_head`, for the h5_shadow.py reason in item 5(3)); at the guard pin all pass on mal-fast-0 (job #1442: `168 passed, 0 failed (job #1442, mal-fast-0, /usr/bin/python3, 2026-10-24T00:54Z; head 0ec7fae8, clean, check tool sha256 1f84072d…, h5_shadow.py blob 23b9ccc2)`); replay: research-0 17 records, old 2 halts, new 0; fast-0 7 records (job #1440), old 1 (the 2026-10-22T19:10Z a2 halt), new 0; other flags unchanged; and `git diff --stat 136cd98..head` empty for `a3_halt.py`, `a3-daily.sh`, `pump_structure_monitor.py`, both pins files and `arena_h5_score.py`. The records PR pins: the check tool blob and sha256, the tests blob and sha256, and the new guard pin. QP OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict, both on the PR, before the merge.

**What does not change**
The promotion gate and its thresholds; §2's deciding cell, one read per `bot_id`, the screen and confirmation steps; the other five A3 flags and Amendment 4's other items (including the fail-closed halt on a PumpSwap redeploy, item 1.14, and the 1.6 no-override rule); the monitor, `a3_halt.py`, `a3-daily.sh` and both pins files (byte-identical to 136cd98); the scorer; EXP-024 Am.4 C1; DEC-024 Am.2; DEC-026; every clock and pinned sheet of a running or registered book. The amendment does not change any book's rule, stake, cell or stack, and it cannot create a pass: it only lowers the chance that a book ends before its read on sampling noise.

**Disclosure**
- **What was in view when this was chosen:** the history of per-run synthetic counts (18 post-redeploy runs, pooled 75/339 = 0.2212), the a2 halt at 8/19, and the replay on PR #840. Neither lapse voids Q50 by itself.
- **Not measured:** no outcome, P&L, fill or exit of any book; no new out-of-sample data; the false-trip and power figures are simulated under a fitted beta-binomial null, not observed; the share is over all 20 graduations of each sample (mayhem pools included), not the book's universe; fast-0's daily file was taken from the manager's facts in the ruling (8/19 and 4/17), not re-read by quant-proof; the Q50 sibling `h5v1-boostfloor` family shares trigger pools, so each read prints Q50 and any a3 revival side by side with the shared-pool count and the better of them is in-sample (Amendment 3 item 8(e), Amendment 5 item 4.3).

### Amendment 11 (written 2026-10-24T17:54:39Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before the clock of `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` (2026-10-25T00:00:00Z, 1792886400000) and before any Q50 attempt count or outcome was seen; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)
DEC-031 Amendment 11: the read instant of `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` counts trades, not attempts (Q50 audit M1; QP-RULING-1024-q50-m1). Scope: this book only, both its screen read and its §2 step 4 confirmation read.
1. **Read instant.** For this book, §2 "When" (and Amendment 4 item 2.3 as it applies here) reads as follows. The read instant is the first 00:00Z boundary B, with clock_start_ms < B <= 2026-11-06T00:00:00Z (the end-seal), such that the attempts decided in [clock_start_ms, B) include at least 100 **counted trades** on at least 5 distinct UTC dates, the dates being the decision dates of those counted trades. A counted trade is a non-refused attempt (Amendment 1; triggers dropped by the Q50 wrapper's s0 margin are not attempts) that has closed and is not excluded under §1.4 or any other exclusion rule that applies to this book. B qualifies only once every attempt decided before B has closed or been excluded (§2, "once all have exited"). The count uses only decision timestamps and the scorer's counted / excluded / open / refused class. It uses and prints no fill, exit price, net, leg or P&L field. A counted trade whose deciding-leg P&L is missing still counts here, and it still makes the read NOT_DECIDABLE (§2, DEC-014 Amendment 1). It never moves the instant.
2. **End.** If no such B exists at or before 2026-11-06T00:00:00Z, the book ENDS NOT_DECIDABLE at the end-seal, final for the bot_id, counts only. There is no early read and no extension.
3. **Confirmation.** For this book, §2 step 4 "at least 100 attempts on at least 5 further UTC dates" reads "at least 100 counted trades (item 1) decided after the screen read's instant, on at least 5 further UTC dates". The read happens at the first 00:00Z boundary that meets this, under whatever end applies to the confirmation.
4. **Unchanged.** The following are unchanged:
   - the promotion gate and every threshold: n >= 100; >= 5 UTC dates with a majority positive; CI90 lower bound > 0 (1,000 draws, seed 1, 5th percentile); ex-top-3 > 0; all under flat 15% and pressure-fail at slope scale 1;
   - the 5% exclusion cap and its denominator (non-refused attempts decided before B), Amendment 6's caps, and DEC-014's NOT_DECIDABLE rules;
   - the deciding cell (1.9 s, 0.30 SOL, rent charged);
   - one read per bot_id, with FAIL and NOT_DECIDABLE final;
   - N and c, frozen_sha, clock, window, H, and the end-seal;
   - DEC-032: C0 still needs a PASS at this read.
   This amendment cannot create a pass. It removes a FAIL decided by count alone (n < 100 after at most 5% exclusions). It moves the read later only by the days needed to add the missing counted trades, by a rule fixed before the clock.
5. **Tools and seal.** No Q50 read runs, and no Q50 ledger row is opened, until both of these hold:
   - a tool that computes item 1 has merged, with quant-proof's OK on its exact head and a reviewer verdict on the PR;
   - the Q50 score loop runs at that commit.
   The tool and the loop must be in place before 2026-10-30T00:00:00Z, the earliest possible boundary (5 dates from the clock). `tools/arena_h5_score.py` stays at sha256 dfe7f018601633921f06668fd1af3aaff7b87bf00f321d1540e10b41f8ab6006. The item-1 count goes in the Q50 wrapper or in the Q50 read tool that is owed under audit B1, and the item-1 instant is recorded in its own write-once file. A `read-instant.json` that the unamended scorer writes for this book is an attempt-boundary record. It is not the read instant, and no seal opens on it. The wrapper's pin change (f5a479ad... to its new sha256) is disclosed at every read it touches (§1.4 pins).
