### Amendment 14 (written 2026-10-24T18:23:15Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before the clock of `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` (2026-10-25T00:00:00Z), before any Q50 halt and before any Q50 attempt count or outcome was seen; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge)
DEC-031 Amendment 14: the Q50 successor policy, fixed before any Q50 halt (quant-proof ruling `hunt29/audit-1024/QP-RULING-1024-successor.md`, sha256 615f31e761bba202a87c42f1473fe6ebb91c70b3c8efd886a9bf3aa2c12de92a, section R0; repo copy `ARTIFACTS/lab/qp-ruling-1024-q50-successor.md`; the halt-class map is its section R1). Scope: `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` and any successor of it.
1. **Q50 successor policy (fixed before any Q50 halt).** If `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` ends by an A3 or dead-man end before any read instant, and its halt class (QP-RULING-1024-successor R1) is H1, H2, H3-resolved, H4a, H5a or H6, a successor IS prepared and registered by the playbook path, without regard to any Q50 outcome. Only these reasons may stop it, each recorded with its instant before the stop: H7 or H4b/H5b class; a kill-review week in [registration, read]; the re-pin, decoder proofs or guard tests not green by D0 + 72 h; a fast-0 memory refusal; a ruling that an item cannot be met. A Q50 outcome, P&L, fill or trade count is never a reason to register or not register a successor, or to pick its class, sha or clock.
2. **Halt classes.** The classes H1 to H7, their conditions and the general conditions are those of the ruling's section R1, copied by reference with that file's sha256. In short: YES for H1, H2, H3 once resolved, H4a, H5a and H6; NO for H4b, H5b and H7. A successor exists only if Q50 ended before any read instant with a NOT_DECIDABLE end. The class is assigned from structure fields only, and it is notebooked before any successor PR opens. A later reclassification may only be stricter.
3. **Disclosure.** Written before the Q50 clock. Not opened: any Q50 shadow output, decision log, ledger, bots.json, score output, count or P&L. The Q50 shadow has run since 2026-10-24T00:21Z, so pre-clock shadow triggers exist; they were not opened. EXP-024 Amendment 10 (a) lets Q50's outcomes be watched in real time. This text is fixed before the clock and before any Q50 outcome was looked at, so that a successor choice is never made with outcomes in view. No book has cleared the promotion gate, and nothing here is a profit claim.

### Amendment 15 (written 2026-10-25T18:47:59Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0 (text copied from the quant-proof ruling); records only; AFTER the HV4 clock 2026-10-25T00:00:00Z and BEFORE any HV4 attempt count, decision row, ledger row or outcome was seen; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge, QP OK and reviewer verdict)
DEC-031 Amendment 15: the read instant of the nine HV4 books carries the exclusion cap as a margin, the trade set of their gate read is defined, and the canary consumer and worst-in-slot step are fixed (QP-RULING-1025-hv4-read-rule, sha256 c2343c9c6ef96256419c4e7f4eec1b83584163bfd3a1aff97ca726cecdc50955). Scope: `hv4-CON-L`, `hv4-CON-F`, `hv4-CON-P`, `hv4-CON-X`, `hv4-CON-R`, `hv4-BIG-L`, `hv4-BIG-F`, `hv4-BIG-X`, `hv4-BIG-R`, at frozen sha 9ed4c5ff, clock 1792886400000; both their screen reads and their §2 step 4 confirmation reads.
1. **Read instant.** For these books, §2 "When" and Am.4 item 2.3 read: B is the first 00:00Z boundary with clock_start_ms < B <= 1794096000000 such that, among the attempts decided in [clock_start_ms, B) and not dropped by Amendment 6 as decided in an excluded hour or restart gap, the attempts of kind `open` (O) and kind `refused`, any reason (R), satisfy O - floor(0.05 x (O + R)) >= 100, and the decision dates of the O attempts cover at least 5 distinct UTC dates. The count uses decision timestamps and row kind only and loads no close, exclusion class, fill, exit, leg or P&L. No such B: the book ENDS NOT_DECIDABLE at 1794096000000, final, counts only.
2. **Trade set.** The gate's n is the attempts of kind `open` decided before B, not dropped, with a priced `trade` row at stake 0.25 and not excluded (§1.4, missing V, late-print gap, engine restart, Am.6 span or open-at-restart). Guarded reverts and strands are trades at their booked value. Refused buys are attempts, never trades. Unfilled landings are inside the fail-model blend and remove nothing. A count-only miss at B is final and labelled "count-only, not a loss"; there is no waiting to a later boundary.
3. **Confirmation.** §2 step 4's "at least 100 attempts on at least 5 further UTC dates" reads "item 1's margin condition over attempts decided after the screen read's instant, on at least 5 further UTC dates", within the same 1794096000000 cut-off.
4. **Tools and seal.** No HV4 read, preview or count runs until a read-instant tool for item 1 and a wrapper that runs `arena_am6_read` (ef6e4555) and `arena_score` (a508ed79, or c4859323 disclosed) unchanged on disk with only the boundary replaced have merged, with QP OK on the exact head and a reviewer verdict, before 2026-10-30T00:00:00Z. A boundary that the unamended tools print is an attempt-boundary record, not the read instant. `tools/hv4_read.py book` is not a gate-read path. If this amendment is not merged by then, §2 applies as registered and this amendment lapses.
5. **Live consumer (DEC-032).** No process reads an HV4 decision row before that bot's read is recorded. After a recorded PASS, only CON-F, CON-X, BIG-F or BIG-X may feed a C0 bridge, under QP-RULING-1025-hv4-read-rule R2.2 (a) to (g), declared in the canary copy before the first send. The L, P and R arms are not canaried under this amendment.
6. **Worst-in-slot.** After a recorded PASS and before any canary proposal, one host-side job exports the scored trade set, runs `tools/arena_worst_gaps.py` and `arena_score.worst_in_slot_cells`, and writes `<bot_id>.wis.json` once. Any missing gap means "worst-in-slot not measured" and no proposal; a worst-in-slot mean <= 0 means no proposal. Report-only; it never changes the verdict.
7. **Unchanged:** the promotion gate and every threshold (n >= 100; >= 5 UTC dates with a majority positive; CI90 lower bound > 0, 1,000 draws, seed 1, 5th percentile; ex-top-3 > 0; flat 15% and pressure-fail at slope scale 1); the deciding cell (1.9 s, 0.25, rent 0 deciding, rent-charged leg beside it); the 5% and 10% caps and their denominators; DEC-014's NOT_DECIDABLE rules and the trim void rule; one read per bot_id; N and c; the frozen sha, clock, H = 14 and END; DESIGN A1 V1/V2 at B + 24 h.
8. **Disclosure.** Written after the clock, with about 18.7 h of the counted window elapsed and unseen. The change moves B only later and only by decision-side counts. It removes a FAIL set by count alone and cannot create a pass. Bias note: O and R are functions of the book's own past outcomes through its caps and loss stops, as under the registered rule. Am.6 drops cluster in busy hours, and the sign of that effect is unknown. Winner's curse: any HV4 pass is the best of the nine siblings and of N arena books, in-sample for that selection. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

*Provenance.* Text copied from the quant-proof ruling `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt30/hv4-read/QP-RULING-1025-hv4-read-rule.md` (full sha256 `c2343c9c6ef96256419c4e7f4eec1b83584163bfd3a1aff97ca726cecdc50955`; also PR #907 comment 6401622729), draft Amendment 15 block. Tools: `tools/hv4_read_instant.py`, `tools/hv4_am15_read.py` (this PR, branch claude/hv4-read-rule-am). Item 6's trade export and `.wis.json` writer are not in this PR (owed, report-only, after a recorded PASS). PR #905's draft Amendment 15 is parked and unmerged; if it is revived it renumbers. No HV4 ledger, count, decision row or output was opened to write this. No book has cleared the promotion gate.


### Amendment 17 (written 2026-10-27T20:09:27Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0, revised 2026-10-27T20:17:24Z on quant-proof's ruling QP-RULING-1027-s1-policy-1058 (comment 6452538754, items P1 to P8) and reviewer comment 6452465227; records only; before any read instant of `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` and before the clock of the book it covers; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge, QP OK on the exact head and a reviewer verdict; number = the next free DEC-031 amendment at merge (Amendment 16 is held by open PR #1019), a renumber is a fill)
DEC-031 Amendment 17: the Q50 successor `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1` is to be registered whatever Q50's verdict or outcome (policy only). It extends Amendment 14 from an A3 or dead-man end before a read to every end of Q50. Scope: `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` and its successor `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1`. The registration path, coverage, read rule and code are NOT fixed here: they are in PR #1057 (draft DEC-031 amendment, the EXP-024 successor declared observation, DEC-032 Amendment 2 Erratum 1, DEC-034), each of which must merge with its own QP OK and reviewer verdict before the successor is registered. No book has cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here is a profit claim.
- **Must merge before 2026-10-30T00:00:00Z** (target 2026-10-29T23:00Z), Q50's earliest read boundary (Amendment 11 item 5), so that it exists before any Q50 read verdict. If it merges later it still binds, and the manager states whether any Q50 outcome or read verdict was looked at before the merge (QP-RULING-1024-successor R0).
- **Disclosure carried by every read of the successor, whatever the merge instant (P7):** "The successor policy (DEC-031 Amendment 17) was fixed at its merge instant, about 2.8 days after Q50's clock (2026-10-25T00:00Z), with Q50's outcomes observable in real time (EXP-024 Amendment 10 (a)). QP-RULING-1024-successor R0's 2026-10-25 deadline covered halt successors only."

1. **The commitment.** Whenever and however `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` ends (its end-seal, a read of any verdict, or an A3 or dead-man end at any instant, before or after a read), unless the end is of class H4b, H5b or H7 (item 2), its successor `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1` is to be registered (by the path of item 4; nothing is registered by this amendment), with:
   - rule `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q50 v1` (`EXP/EXP-024-RULE-Q50.md`, sha256 `b3f8ecbf8731dc322a035a0b3996b7ed3f0b8c508cc6947014e3de1aa9524fd4`), Q50's deciding cell (1.9 s leg at 0.30 SOL, rent charged, both fail models) and Q50's stack;
   - `frozen_sha` `5c2ab7c64b21358619e37068c6a53e4d183512ea` (S\*, tag `q50-succ-base-1`; QP-RULING-1024-successor R7(b)). Not Q50's `a5f41370`, which seals every pool with s0 at or after 2026-11-06T00Z in fresh-forward mode (R7(a));
   - **clock.** If Q50 has had no A3 or dead-man end before 2026-11-04T00:00:00Z: `clock_start_ms` **1793923200000 (2026-11-06T00:00:00Z)**, Q50's end-seal, so that the two windows are disjoint (Amendment 3 item 5) and there is no gap. If Q50 ends by an A3 or dead-man end of a class that allows a successor, at any instant (before or after a read): the first 00:00Z after the last pre-clock item of Amendment 14's playbook path, and never before 2026-11-06T00Z unless Q50's window has already ended. Either way, if a pre-clock item is not done in time, the clock is the first 00:00Z after the last item (slip), never an earlier one;
   - **P0 and H.** For a clock at or before 2026-11-07T00:00:00Z, Q50's P0 (job #1413, rate 35.333333333333336, `ARTIFACTS/lab/arena-records-2026-10-24-q50.md` lines 11 and 28) is reused (R3.4), and H = 14 (Amendment 4 item 2.2). For a later clock (P1), a new structure-only P0 on 3 full closed UTC days (Amendment 4 item 2.1, at S\*'s rule module or the QP-ruled proxy, fixed-first text notebooked before the count, the floor of Amendment 4 item 2.1 applies) is pre-committed here, H is recomputed from it by Amendment 4 item 2.2, capped by `SUCC_END_MS`, and never extended.
   - **Slip record (P1).** Each slip is notebooked with the unmet item and its instant before the clock it misses. Every later read of this rule (the successor's, and any further successor's) lists each pre-clock build item's merge instant beside Q50's read instant (or Q50's end instant if it had no read).

   **Q50's screen read, confirmation read, verdict (PASS, FAIL, NOT_DECIDABLE or unread), outcome, P&L, fill or trade count is never a reason to register or not register the successor, or to pick its sha, clock, stake, H, window or read.**
2. **The only stop reasons**, each recorded in a notebook entry with its instant before the stop. Nothing else stops the commitment; work not yet done is a slip (item 1), never a stop.
   - **Class H4b, H5b or H7** (QP-RULING-1024-successor R1) of an A3 flag, or of the A3 or dead-man end of Q50 or of a registered successor, on any guard run before the successor's read. The class is assigned from structure fields only (the verdict reasons; the fee-tier and account-field evidence for H4a versus H4b and H5a versus H5b; the BOOST flags for H7), notebooked with those evidence fields, and confirmed by quant-proof before it stops the successor. A reclassification after a Q50 read verdict exists may only be stricter, and only with quant-proof (P6 (ii)).
   - **A kill-review week** whose dates are recorded on main before this amendment merges and overlap [registration, read] (P2). None is recorded at writing (LAB_STATE schedules none). A kill-review week recorded later is not a stop reason under this item.
   - **A memory refusal, only on the second (P3).** A first fast-0 memory refusal of the successor's shadow job is a slip: the job is retried with the recorded `MemoryMax` after Q50's shadow is cancelled, and the clock moves to the next 00:00Z (2026-11-07T00:00Z at the latest for the 11-06 path). It is a stop only if the job is refused again for that later clock, with `MemoryCurrent` and the request recorded both times.
   - **A quant-proof ruling of external impossibility (P4):** that an item cannot be met for a reason outside the lab's control (a seal, a proven code impossibility, or a host). An item not yet built, reviewed or merged is never such a reason.
3. **Mechanics by structure, not by outcome (P5, P6).**
   - If Q50 ends by an A3 or dead-man end of class H1, H2, H3-resolved, H4a, H5a or H6, before or after a read, the successor follows the mechanics of Amendment 14's playbook: the re-pin, a new guard pin, a new state file (R4.2) and the R10.1 order. Amendment 14's and R1.1's condition "before any read instant, with a NOT_DECIDABLE end" does not apply to this commitment.
   - Which mechanics apply is decided by structure only: Amendment 14's playbook if the guard's state holds a sticky non-PASS entry (checked from the applied log's verdict fields); item 1's 11-06 path otherwise.
   - If a guard run ends a registered successor before its clock, the successor ends NOT_DECIDABLE with an empty window, adds 1 to N, and its own successor follows R8.4 under this same item.
4. **What this does not do, and the bar on later texts (P8).** It registers nothing and authorises no process, no live send and no coverage of any pool: the successor's process, registration and read wait for the texts of #1057 (or their replacements) to merge. Those texts, and every later text about the successor's paper book, may not add any condition that depends on a Q50 outcome or verdict to its registration, sha, clock, H, window or read. The live rung carry-over (DEC-032 Amendment 2 item 12.3 and DEC-034 route R-C) depends on Q50's confirmation PASS by design; it is a live-rung rule only and never touches the paper book. After a Q50 FAIL, a successor C0 canary on its own screen PASS (DEC-034 route R-0) does not use the standing C0 owner line of DEC-032 Amendment 1 item 3: the owner is asked afresh, with Q50's FAIL stated in the question (QP-RULING-1027-s1 (c)). A Q50 FAIL stays final for Q50's `bot_id` (§2); the successor is a new `bot_id`, adds 1 to N (Amendment 4 item 2.3), and its read prints Q50's verdict beside it (Amendment 3 item 8 (e)).
5. **Unchanged:** the promotion gate and every threshold; §2; Amendments 1 to 16; Q50's sha, clock, window, H, end-seal and read; EXP-024 and its seals; DEC-032.
6. **Disclosure.** Written with no Q50 or HV4 output, ledger, `bots.json`, score, count, A3 record, job status or log opened. `docs/HANDOFF.md` §1 quotes Q50's day-1 dry counts; they were in view as text and used for nothing. Q50's outcomes have been observable in real time since 2026-10-25 (EXP-024 Amendment 10 (a)); this writer looked at none. Not a profit claim.

DEC-031 Amendment 18: the registration path of the 2026-11-06 successor of `h5v1q50-boostfloor-a1` (Q50), so that the BOOST-floor line keeps forward-testing from Q50's end-seal with no gap, under DEC-032 Amendment 2 item 12 route (a) and QP-RULING-1024-successor (`ARTIFACTS/lab/qp-ruling-1024-q50-successor.md`, source sha256 `615f31e761bba202a87c42f1473fe6ebb91c70b3c8efd886a9bf3aa2c12de92a`) R3, R7, R8 and R10. Scope: `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1` only. No book has cleared the promotion gate. Q50 and this successor have 0 read out-of-sample trades. Q* = 50 is a discarded discovery cell with no edge figure of its own (Amendment 5 item 4.4). Nothing here is a profit claim.
- Takes effect on merge, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict on the PR (merge instant: the committer time of the merge commit on origin/main, as in Amendment 2 item 2). The policy (the commitment to register, the sha, the clock and its slip, H and the stop reasons) is Amendment 17 (PR #1058), which merges on its own before 2026-10-30T00:00:00Z. This amendment adds the registration path and may not add any condition that depends on a Q50 outcome or verdict (Amendment 17 item 3).
- It must merge before the successor's registration (item 4), and in any case with at least 48 h of slack before 2026-11-06T00:00:00Z (target 2026-11-03T00:00:00Z).

1. **Policy: Amendment 17 (#1058), by reference.** Its items 1 to 4 (the commitment whatever Q50's verdict, the closed stop list, mechanics by structure, and the bar on any Q50-dependent condition for the paper book) bind this amendment. Where an item below restates a value Amendment 17 fixes (sha, clock, slip, P0, H), Amendment 17 wins. Every read of the successor carries Amendment 17's disclosure line (its P7 line).
2. **The book.**
   1. **bot_id `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1`.** QP-RULING-1024-successor R8.1 fixed the successor scheme `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s<k>`, with `s1` the first successor whichever way Q50 ends. The `-a<k>` suffix names a fresh registration of an ended book (the 10-23 `-a2` cohort, Amendment 10 item 4's `-a3` revivals), not a continuation, so it is not used. The id keeps Amendment 5 item 2.1's three properties (starts `h5v1`; does not start `h5v1-boostfloor-`; starts `h5`), shown by a committed test against `tools/arena_h5_score.set_window_end`'s prefixes and `tools/a3_halt.validate_books` (owed; plan item C1).
   2. **Rule** `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q50 v1`, `EXP/EXP-024-RULE-Q50.md` sha256 `b3f8ecbf8731dc322a035a0b3996b7ed3f0b8c508cc6947014e3de1aa9524fd4`, exactly Q50's.
   3. **frozen_sha S\* = `5c2ab7c64b21358619e37068c6a53e4d183512ea`** (tag `q50-succ-base-1` on origin; never merged to main, PR #896 closed by design). **Not F50 (`a5f41370`).** At F50 the fresh-forward engine seals every pool with s0 at or after 2026-11-06T00:00Z (`tools/h5_shadow.py` `_h5_sealed_now`, F50 line 2215; call site 3247), so a book at F50 can never score a pool after Q50's end (QP-RULING-1024-successor R7(a): DEC-032 Amendment 2 item 12.1 "the same `frozen_sha` as Q50" cannot coexist with 12.2(a); corrected by DEC-032 Amendment 2 Erratum 1). S\* differs from F50 in one non-test file only, `tools/h5_shadow.py` (blob `f23cf492` -> `7f859b12deef87573a7314e49e125b5121eaa645`, file sha256 `09056ff61c7bec0bed38c97754b307383fee34c5b77bdf43c99d69197f2f8ffa`): a new constant `SUCC_END_MS` = 2026-12-01T00:00:00Z (1796083200000) used only when the engine runs fresh-forward (`fresh_start_ms` set), at the seal test, the fresh call site and the start record's `end_only_ms`; `H5_LOOK2_END_MS` and every non-fresh path are byte-identical (R7(b).1). Proofs at S\*, as recorded in the tag message (the manager re-verifies each id on its PR or job before registration): md5 decision-equivalence job #1548 VERDICT EQUAL on 2026-09-20 (Run A default `2365a84d`/`00aa373f`, Run B `--q-star-sol 50` `71de8168`/`66e69a69`); `tools/test_h5_shadow_succ_identity.py` at S\* (AST identity of every function vs F50 except the changed lines; the seal grid on synthetic 2026 times; a `run_live`-built engine end to end at Q\* 40 and 50); QP OK comment 6388683530; reviewer PASS. Any other sha is a new `bot_id`.
   4. **Stake, stack and legs as Q50.** Deciding cell: the 1.9 s leg at 0.30 SOL, rent charged, both fail models, strand rules sc and total; the stack is DEC-031-rev2 (505,000 lamports per send; Amendment 8 does not cover it, item 3 of Amendment 8). Exit v1 (330 s).
3. **P0 and H.**
   1. **P0: Q50's P0 reused (QP-RULING-1024-successor R3.1), disclosed.** Job #1413 (`j_Af3reOXXEdqtjg`), deciding cell `G0_wsol_band|trig|Q50|strict`, 2026-10-21 46, 10-22 25, 10-23 35, mean 35.333333333333336, min day 25 (`ARTIFACTS/lab/arena-records-2026-10-24-q50.md` lines 11 and 28). Disclosure (R3.2): a proxy counter on arena tape, a different feed from the shadow's; an upper bound (synthetic and unclassified pools included); pre-redeploy October days; day 3 a lower bound; the old Amendment 3 bar; the trigger rate on the shadow's own feed after 2026-10-23 is unmeasured. No count of Q50's records is made or used (R3.3). The reuse is valid only for a clock at or before 2026-11-07T00:00:00Z (R3.4).
   2. **H = min(90, max(14, ceil(150 / 35.333333333333336))) = min(90, max(14, 5)) = 14 days** (Amendment 4 item 2.2). The book's end is **E_s1 = clock + 14 d = 2026-11-20T00:00:00Z (1795132800000)**, below `SUCC_END_MS`, which is only the coverage cap. H is never extended (Amendment 4 items 2.2, 2.3).

It is also copied in the registration notebook entry and the records PR. A later A3 or dead-man end may only move it earlier.
4. **Clock: 2026-11-06T00:00:00Z (1793923200000), exactly Q50's end-seal.**
   - **Why not earlier:** Q50's window is [2026-10-25T00Z, 2026-11-06T00Z). A clock inside it puts a second book of the same rule beside the parent on the same pools, which Amendment 3 item 5 forbids. At 11-06T00Z the two windows are disjoint and half-open.
   - **Why not later:** any later clock is a gap in the line, and the P0 reuse expires after 2026-11-07T00:00:00Z.
   - **Registration** (the `bots.json` line and its notebook copy) is made between 2026-11-04T00:00Z and 2026-11-05T12:00Z, outside :04 to :25 of 07, 13 and 19 UTC and outside 12:10 to 12:25Z. Amendment 3 item 1 makes the clock the first 00:00Z after registration, "Item 4, or another rule of this DEC, can put it later. It is never earlier": this item puts it at 2026-11-06T00:00:00Z.
   - **Slip (Amendment 17 item 1).** If any pre-clock item of item 8 is not done by 2026-11-05T23:00:00Z, the clock is the first 00:00Z after the last item: 2026-11-07T00:00:00Z (window [11-07, 11-21), E_s1 = 1795219200000) with the P0 reuse, or a later 00:00Z with the new structure-only P0 that Amendment 17 pre-commits and H recomputed from it. The slip is notebooked with the unmet item before the clock it misses. If the `bots.json` line was already written with the missed clock, that book ends NOT_DECIDABLE before its clock (empty window, N + 1) and `-s2` is registered by the same rule. Nothing is cut to keep a date.
5. **Coverage (DEC-032 Amendment 2 item 12.2 (a)).**

   2. **§3.5 successor end-seal (QP-RULING-1024-successor R7(d), text as ruled, value filled):** "§3.5, successor end-seal for `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q50 v1` successors. The 2026-11-06T00Z end-seal is unchanged for every other book, for the Q50 process at F50, and for the executor until its own note and md5 proof merge."
   3. **Executor (live path only; DEFERRED; not on the clock's critical path).** The executor's `SEAL_END_MS` (`tools/h5_arena_executor.py:74`, 2026-11-06T00Z) is **not raised**: raising it would widen Q50's executor observation past the Note under Amendment 10. Checked at origin/main: `DEC/DEC-031-forward-paper-arena.md`, `DEC/DEC-032-arena-canary-template.md`, `DEC/DEC-034-h5v1q50-s1-canary.md`, `EXP/EXP-024-note-s1-successor.md` and `docs/` are not on that list; `EXP/EXP-024-h5-boostfloor-part1-prereg.md`, `tools/h5_arena_executor.py`, `tools/h5_arena_ladder.py`, `tools/h5_arena_exec_replay.py` and `tools/h5_shadow.py` are, and none of them is edited before that point. 6. **A3.**
   1. The successor is registered after Amendments 4 and 10 merged, so it is listed under `new_rule_bot_ids` and the Amendment 10 pooled synthetic rule applies to it. **Books file v3** = v2 with `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1` appended to `new_rule_bot_ids`; it is swapped in the same command as registration (Amendment 5 item 2.2; DEC-032 Amendment 3 item 3's registry hygiene); its sha256 and a verbatim copy go in the notebook copy and the records PR. **Q50's reads keep the v2 view (QP-RULING-1027-s1 (e)2):** before the swap the manager keeps a byte-copy of books v2 (sha256 `819c699d…`), and every Q50 run of `tools/arena_h5_read.py` and `tools/arena_h5_legs.py` runs from a worktree at a pinned commit before the books-v3 code change (plan C4), with that v2 copy; disclosing a change is not enough where it can be avoided.    2. **No loop redeploy.** The running loop at guard pin `a3-guard-pin-1024` (`0ec7fae8`; #1446, then #1447) judges the successor from its registration. If Q50's state file (`$A3_OUT/a3-redeploy-state.jsonl`) holds any sticky non-PASS entry at registration, this item does not apply: Q50 has then ended under Amendment 4, the halt-successor path of Amendment 14 decides, and a new state file and guard pin are mandatory (R4.2, R10.1). The manager checks this from the applied log's verdict fields only (structure), at the swap and again at the pre-clock green run (item 6.4); a non-PASS in [registration, clock) ends the successor NOT_DECIDABLE before its clock (empty window, N + 1). quant-proof accepted the shared state file and loop (QP-RULING-1027-s1, answer 2).

On the manager's facts in DEC-032 Amendment 3 item 7 this needs loop job 3 after #1447, dead-man job 3 after #1333 and the alert #1540 extension or successor; each is submitted at least 48 h before its predecessor's actual limit and in any case before the registration.

So: the read instant is the first 00:00Z boundary B with 1793923200000 < B <= 1795132800000 such that the attempts decided in [clock, B) include at least 100 counted trades on at least 5 distinct UTC decision dates, once every attempt decided before B has closed or been excluded; none by E_s1 means NOT_DECIDABLE at E_s1, final, counts only; the confirmation is at least 100 counted trades decided after the screen read's instant on at least 5 further UTC dates, by E_s1. 8. **Process and pre-clock list.** `tools/h5_shadow.py` at S\*, from a clean checkout, `--fresh-forward ARENA --q-star-sol 50` (bx10 is off at Q\* 50 in code), its own out dir under `$HOME/data/arena/h5-q50s1-*`, its own `MemoryMax`, started after `registered_at_ms` and at least 1 h before the clock with a clean log (R10.2), as late as that allows: **target start 2026-11-05 20:00 to 22:30Z** (QP-RULING-1027-s1 (e)3). **Memory (an OOM would end Q50's open positions NOT_DECIDABLE, Amendment 3 item 8 (j), and gap-halt Q50's live feed):** every new process (the smoke, the successor shadow) has its own `MemoryMax`, and before each start `systemctl show user-1002.slice -p MemoryCurrent` plus the sum of all running and new `MemoryMax` values must fit the slice; otherwise the start waits (a slip under Amendment 17 item 2, never a reason to start uncapped). Q50's shadow keeps running until Q50's last exit has been written (about 2026-11-06T00:07Z), then is cancelled as R10.3 recommends (queued job first; output dir kept, never opened for the successor). Before the clock: the `bots.json` line and notebook copy; books v3; the green run of item 6.4; the shadow's start record (code_sha S\*, `fresh_forward` ARENA, `dirty` false, `tip_union` off, rule and `rule_sha256` as item 2, `q_star_sol` 50.0, `end_only_ms` 1796083200000); the day-1 dry-score job (counts only) and the halt-alert job submitted; the kill-review line; the A3 chain of item 6.7. The records PR follows within 24 h of registration.
   - **Amendment 6 (smoke-then-clock) applies** (the registration is after its merge): unit tests green at S\* (cited in the records PR); one live smoke of at least 2 h at S\* with the book's flags in a scratch root, after the EXP-024 successor note merges (its part A covers that smoke, never counted or read), judged only on the blocking list notebooked before it starts; the Amendment 6 integrity report tool and per-hour thresholds pinned in the registration record (proposed: Q50's own pins, cited by path@sha256, so the two books' excluded hours are computed alike). A smoke FAIL means no clock until a smoke passes at S\*; a new sha would be a new `bot_id`.
9. **N, c and siblings (R8).** N + 1 at the successor's clock; Q50 stays in N with its own end. A successor screen PASS enters confirmation automatically and adds 1 to c (Amendment 3 item 2). No carry-over into the paper read: no Q50 trade is pooled, no confirmation credit passes (R8.2), and the successor's read is never Q50's confirmation (§2 step 4 and Amendment 3 item 2 need the same `bot_id`; Amendment 3 item 5). Every successor read prints side by side, counts only: Q50 (its read verdict or end class, counted trades), `h5v1-boostfloor-a2` and `h5v2-boostclock-a2` (ended, 0 attempts), and any other successor, with the shared-pool count (Amendment 3 item 8 (e)). A successor pass is best of N, in-sample against Q50, and, after a Q50 FAIL or NOT_DECIDABLE, the second sample of a rule whose first sample did not pass: at about 5% per look the rule-level false-pass chance is about 1 - 0.95^2 = 9.75%, which the uncorrected screen does not show, and the read says so. If Q50 also entered confirmation, both count in c (c counts tests). Live rung carry-over is DEC-032 Amendment 2 item 12.3 as corrected by its Erratum 1, in the successor's own canary copy (DEC-034); it changes nothing in this paper book.
10. **Unchanged:** the promotion gate and every threshold (n >= 100; >= 5 UTC dates with a majority positive; CI90 lower bound > 0, 1,000 draws, seed 1, 5th percentile; ex-top-3 > 0; flat 15% and pressure-fail at slope scale 1); §2's one read per `bot_id` and its deciding cell; the 5% and Amendment 6 caps and denominators; DEC-014's NOT_DECIDABLE rules; Q50's sha, clock, window, H, end-seal, A3 rule, state file, wrapper, instant tool and read; EXP-024 and its seals (Look 2 to its end state, first 2026-11-14T00:00Z); Amendments 1 to 17; every other registered book.

### Amendment 19 (DRAFT; number = the next free DEC-031 amendment at merge, Amendment 16 is held by open PR #1019, a renumber is a fill; written 2026-10-27T21:21:00Z, revised 2026-10-27T21:32:12Z on quant-proof's comment 6454061741 (B1 to B6, rulings (a) to (f)) and its B1 correction 6454095235, both from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before the registration, P0 count and clock of the book it covers, and before any outcome of that book exists; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge, QP OK on the exact head and a reviewer verdict)
DEC-031 Amendment 19: the decision to register the Q* = 60 sibling `h5v1q60-boostfloor-a1` (rule `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q60 v1`; library row `l3-boost-h5q50-1` variant B) as its own arena book on its own clock, and the rules it runs under. Scope: this book, plus one report-only print line in the successor `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1`'s read (item 7). No book has cleared the promotion gate.

Q* = 60 is a discarded discovery cell. Discovery was monotone, with smaller Q\* better, so 60 is the worst of {40, 50, 60}. No Q60 edge figure exists, and none exists for Q50 either. Nothing here is a profit claim.

Companion texts:
- `EXP/EXP-024-RULE-Q60.md` (this PR).
- `docs/research/q60-sibling/PLAN.md` (this PR).
- The declared observation `EXP/EXP-024-note-q60.md`, which comes in a **later PR** (item 5).

**Takes effect** on merge, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict on the PR. **Its squash-merge committer time must be before 2026-10-30T00:00:00Z**, Q50's earliest read boundary, so that the registration decision exists before any Q50 read verdict (QP ruling (c)).
- If it merges later, every Q60 read states: "the Q60 registration was decided after Q50's earliest read instant, with Q50's outcomes and any Q50 read verdict observable."
- A Q50 or s1 outcome, verdict, P&L, fill or count is never a reason to register or not register this book, to stop it, or to pick its sha, clock, stake, H, window or read.

**Pinned set.** Nothing in this work changes any of the 43 paths of `scripts/ops/q50-xtime-manifest.sh` line 23 (the manager's rule of 2026-10-27; QP ruling (d) verified 0 of this PR's paths on it). The Q60 code lives on a tagged commit off S\*, never merged to main.

1. **The book.**
   - **bot_id** `h5v1q60-boostfloor-a1`. It has Amendment 5 item 2.1's three properties: it starts `h5v1`, does not start `h5v1-boostfloor-`, and starts `h5`. A committed test shows them (plan Q1). `-a1` marks a first registration of a rule; `-s<k>` is for successors.
   - **Rule** `H5-BOOSTFLOOR-Q60 v1`, `EXP/EXP-024-RULE-Q60.md`, sha256 `f36afaae00edd5b8292fdbe732c6034251cc1371afb6c1e54b50e548a91717b9`. It is the Q50 block except four lines (1, 22, 42, 47), as quant-proof verified.
   - **Deciding cell, as Q50's** (Amendment 5 item 2.3): the 1.9 s leg at 0.30 SOL, rent charged, both fail models, strand rules sc and total. The stack is DEC-031-rev2 at 505,000 lamports per send; Amendment 8 does not cover it. Exit v1 (330 s).
2. **Frozen sha.** `q60_sha` is defined here as **the commit tagged `q60-base-1` on origin, whose diff against S\* (`5c2ab7c64b21358619e37068c6a53e4d183512ea`, tag `q50-succ-base-1`) is exactly the following and nothing else.**

   The changes, in `tools/h5_shadow.py`:
   - (a) `Q_STAR_ALLOWED` = (40.0, 50.0, 60.0).

### Amendment 20 (DRAFT; number = the next free DEC-031 amendment at merge: Amendment 16 is held by open PR #1019, Amendments 17, 18 and 19 are on main (Amendment 19 merged from PR #1064, main `c80e2ac9`); a renumber at merge is a fill; written 2026-10-27T21:35:12Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; before the DBC m10 confirmation read prints LOOK CONSUMED, before any count of that read's window, and before any P18 `bot_id`, P0, `bots.json` line or clock exists; outcome-blind; status: DRAFT until merge, QP OK on the exact head and a reviewer verdict)
DEC-031 Amendment 20: **B'**, one declared departure from Amendment 3 item 1 and Amendment 4 item 2.1 (the P0 source) for the DBC P18 forward paper book, the copy of m10 specified in PR #950 `docs/research/p33-dbc-p18/STAGING.md` §1 (head `2ac6fca00ad0dc36f9cfec7b36f7fd1e5e58e6cc`). Under B', P18's P0 is the DBC m10 confirmation read's own R13 per-UTC-day attempt counts, not 3 new closed days of the live process (Plan B). That moves P18's earliest clock from 2026-11-06T00:00Z (Plan B) to 2026-11-04T00:00Z. This turns quant-proof's ruling QP-RULING-1026-p33-p18 Q2 (a) to (e) into DEC text, in the manner of Amendment 5 item 1, and does not change its substance. Ruling: `ARTIFACTS/lab/qp-ruling-1026-p33-p18.md`, a byte-identical copy (sha256 `1de03ab381274be2bd79b2c5b021657a53a74c81d9498a31b01dfe8283f06c90`) of `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt30/p33/QP-RULING-1026-p33-p18.md`, posted as quant-proof's comment 6423110733 on PR #950 (verdict OK at `2ac6fca0`). **Nothing is registered by this amendment:** no `bot_id`, `bots.json` line, N increment, P0 run or registration record (ruling Q1 item 4). No book has cleared the promotion gate. m10 has 0 out-of-sample trades and P18 has none. Nothing here is a profit claim.
- **Takes effect on merge**, after quant-proof's OK on the exact final head and a reviewer verdict on the PR. The merge instant is the committer time of the merge commit on origin/main (Amendment 2 item 2).
- **Deadline (ruling Q2(a)).** B' is available only if the merge instant is before **2026-11-01T00:10:00Z**, the earliest instant at which the read can print LOOK CONSUMED (REGISTRATION R1 and R14; the read job waits for 00:10Z). This is stricter than "before LOOK CONSUMED" on purpose: it can be checked from the merge commit alone, with no job-log timestamp. Target: merged by 2026-10-31T00:00Z. If the merge instant is later, this amendment has no effect and Plan B applies (item 4). The choice between B and B' is made by this rule, at this merge, and never with the read's counts in view.

1. **Applies only on a PASS.** B' applies only if the registered read (`python3 -m tools.dbc_fwd_read_am1 --confirm-one-look` at tag `dbc-m10-forward-v1-am1`, run by `scripts/research/dbc-m10-read-job.sh`) writes `verdict` PASS in `fwd-m10-am1.json`, and the manager has recorded that verdict in a notebook entry. On FAIL, NOT_DECIDABLE, PASS-SCREEN-LOOKAHEAD, any other verdict or no verdict, **nothing is registered**: no P0 is taken from the read, the P18 process stops at once, and Plan B does not apply either (STAGING §0; ruling Q1, "After the read"). N does not change.
2. **P0 source (the departure; ruling Q2(b)).**
   1. **The field.** The key path `deciding.attempts_by_day` of `/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt29/dbc/fwd-read-v1-am1/fwd-m10-am1.json`: R13's "attempts per day" of the AM1 deciding set. In code (`tools/dbc_fwd_read_am1.py` line 163 at main `a31d9864`) it counts every sealed causal decision of `tools/dbc_fwd.Engine` (the list `c1`, appended at trigger time and never removed) by `tools/dbc_screen.day_of` of its block time. The line runs after pricing in code order, but its value comes from the sealed decision list alone and holds no priced value. Only this field gives the counts. **Not used:** `per_day_n`, `per_day` or any other field under `primary_1p9s`, `entry_2p5s` or `screen_report_only`. Those are priced lines and sit next to SOL figures. `fwd-m10-am1.md` does not print `attempts_by_day`, so the JSON is the source.
   2. **When and how.** The field is copied only after LOOK CONSUMED and after the verdict is recorded (item 1). It is copied verbatim into the registration notebook entry and the records PR by a key-path extraction (for example `jq -c '.deciding.attempts_by_day'`), never by hand. The same entry cites the JSON's sha256 as printed in the last line of `fwd-m10-am1.md`.
   3. **Days.** Exactly the 7 UTC dates 2026-10-25 to 2026-10-31. A date missing from the field counts as 0. A key outside those 7 dates makes B' NOT_DECIDABLE (item 4).
   4. **Fixed first.** The rule text is items 2 to 5 of this amendment, merged before the look. The registration notebook entry cites it by merge commit before the counts are copied into it.
3. **Floor, bar and gap rule.**
   1. **Floor (Amendment 4 item 2.1, unchanged):** a mean of at least 2.0 would-be attempts a day, and no day at 0.
   2. **Proxy bar (ruling Q2(c); the Amendment 5 item 1.3 bar).** The proxy is not conservative. It is a backfill with blockTime that counts every sealed decision, while the live process runs on receive time and can drop rows, so it is an upper bound on the live count. B' passes only if the mean of the 7 day counts is **at least 20** and **each of the 7 days is at least 10**. That bar implies the floor.
   3. **Gap rule** (the hard-gap rule of Amendment 3 item 1, kept by Amendment 4 item 2.1, carried to a backfill). B' passes only if the read's own `hours_present` equals `hours_needed` (168) and `bad_hours` is empty. Any missing or incomplete window hour makes B' NOT_DECIDABLE. This is stricter than the read's own rule (more than 6 bad hours = NOT_DECIDABLE, REGISTRATION R3) on purpose: the read may tolerate bad hours for its verdict, but a P0 window with a hole is not accepted. These fields are data completeness (R13 "hours present"), structure only. They are copied in the same extraction as item 2.2. This is the one field use beyond ruling Q2(b)'s "only that field", and it is put to quant-proof for a ruling on this PR.
   4. **Day 1 is a lower bound.** R5's warm-up hour (`FIRST_OK` 2026-10-25T01:00Z) gives 2026-10-25 23 eligible hours, so its count is a lower bound for a full day. That is the conservative direction for an "at least" bar. It is not adjusted.
4. **Below the bar, or a gap: NOT_DECIDABLE for B', not a drop.** P18 then goes to Plan B: P0 = the live process's own would-be attempts on 3 full closed UTC days at the registration-candidate sha (Amendment 4 item 2.1, its floor and gap rule; the Amendment 5 live-P0 route), earliest clock 2026-11-06T00:00Z. There is no re-count, no other field, no other window and no second use of the read's output for P0. Plan B applies the same way if this amendment misses its deadline, and if the B' clock would fall after 2026-11-06T00:00:00Z (item 6, expiry).
5. **Horizon (ruling Q2(d)).** `rate` = the sum of the 7 day counts divided by 7, exact. `H = min(90, max(14, ceil(150 / rate)))` (Amendment 4 item 2.2). **H = 14 for any rate of at least 150/14 = 10.714285714285714 a day** (the exact value governs). Under item 3.2's bar, `rate` is at least 20, so `ceil(150 / rate)` is at most 8 and H = 14 in every case where B' applies. A PASS also implies at least 100 fills in 7 days, about 14.29 a day at least. H = 14 is pinned in the `bots.json` line (or under Amendment 7) and its notebook copy before the clock. It is never extended, and it is further capped by any end-seal or window end that applies to the book (Amendment 4 item 2.2); the registration names any that applies. At the earliest clock the window is [2026-11-04T00:00Z, 2026-11-18T00:00Z).
6. **Clock.** **2026-11-04T00:00:00Z (`clock_start_ms` 1793750400000)** if every pre-clock item below lands in time. Amendment 3 item 1 makes the clock the first 00:00Z after the registration record (the `bots.json` line and its notebook copy), so the record must be in before 2026-11-04T00:00Z. **If any item is late, the clock is the next 00:00Z after the last item.** Nothing is cut to keep a date. B' waives no item.
   - **Expiry.** B' applies only if the clock is at or before **2026-11-06T00:00:00Z (1793923200000)**, Plan B's earliest clock. If any pre-clock item would put it later, B' lapses mechanically and item 4 (Plan B, live P0) applies. Nobody chooses. (B' exists only to start the clock earlier than Plan B; after that instant its 10-25 to 10-31 blockTime proxy is stale beside a live P0 on receive time at the registration sha. Amendment 17 put an expiry on the Q50 P0 reuse for the same reason.)
   - (a) This amendment merged before the deadline above.
   - (b) The read's PASS recorded; the field copied; the bar and the gap rule met (items 1 to 3).
   - (c) The live P18 process at the registration-candidate sha started after the read printed LOOK CONSUMED (ruling Q1 item 3), early enough that 2026-11-02 is a full closed UTC day for it (in practice before 2026-11-01T23:00Z, with one warm-up hour).
   - (d) The day-1 dry score of that live process on 2026-11-02 passes every item, with the bars fixed before the process starts: STAGING §4 items 1 to 5, ruling Q4.3 (item 6, L90 <= 2.5 s) and ruling Q6 (item 7, RSS; item 8, credits). That includes item 4, the live-vs-backfill cross-check after 2026-11-02's backfill, which is the only link between the proxy and the process (ruling Q2(e)). One process per sha. A sha change after the dry score needs a new dry score.
   - (e) B1 to B3 merged, each with its own QP OK and reviewer verdict (ruling, "Conditions for this OK"), with the registration pack: frozen sha and sha256 list, the A3-style guard of DESIGN §3 item 4, the parity statement and the multiplicity text of item 7.
   - (f) A kill-review week check line in the registration notebook entry (ruling Q6). A kill-review week stops the registration.
   - (g) The `bots.json` line and its notebook copy, with H pinned.
7. **Multiplicity.** **N + 1** at P18's clock (Amendment 4 item 2.5). The registration and every P18 read state: m10 is the best of 23 screened DBC cells; the Swap3-era rehearsal day (2026-10-16) came out at about one-fifth of the Swap2-era mean; the receive-time differences of STAGING §1; and that P0 came from the read under this departure. A P18 screen pass enters confirmation automatically and adds 1 to c (Amendment 3 item 2). The read's own trades never count toward P18's gate: P18 counts only attempts decided at or after its `clock_start_ms` (§1.2).
8. **The read is not changed in any way.** REGISTRATION R1 to R14, AMENDMENT-1 AM1 to AM6, READ-HARDENING, the tag `dbc-m10-forward-v1-am1`, every pin (the 12 paths of ruling Q1 item 2, `tools/dbc_m10_read_ops.py` and `scripts/research/dbc-m10-read-job.sh`), the marker v3, the output directory, the verdict function and every bar stay as they are. This PR changes no code. The read does not read, import or depend on this amendment. B' only names one field of the read's output (and the completeness fields of item 3.3) to be copied after its verdict. DESIGN §3's line "the P0 days must lie OUTSIDE the backfill-read window (R3 forbids counting window hours)" is superseded for P18 under B' only. R3 bars any count of a window hour before the read. Under B' no count is run on window hours at all: the read's own printed field is quoted after the look.
9. **Why the departure is acceptable (independence).** P0 decides two things only: the floor and H.
   - **P0 counts are structure, not outcome.** They are decision timestamps of sealed decisions, taken from the sealed decision list with no fill, price or P&L in them, as DEC-031 §2's decision-timestamp counter is.
   - **Disclosed: exit-timing dependence.** The live cooldown is `busy_live = exit_bt + cooldown_s` (`tools/dbc_fwd.py:470`), with `exit_bt` from `exit_fire`. So the number of decisions per pool depends on when the previous exit fired, which is path information. It is not a fill or P&L, and Plan B's live attempt count has exactly the same property.
   - **Disclosed: a further upper bound.** `attempts_by_day` also counts decisions that AM2 later excludes (block_edge, bad_hour), so it is a further upper bound on priced attempts. The bar of item 3.2 is an "at least" bar, so its direction is unchanged.
   - **Given a PASS, the proxy cannot move either decision.** A PASS means at least 100 fills in 7 days, so the rate is above 10.714 a day and H = 14; the floor is met.
   - **The only effect the counts can have** is to send P18 to Plan B (item 4): a 2-day delay. They never change P18's rule, pins, bars, stake, deciding cell or read. Those are fixed before the look (ruling Q1, "After the read").
   - **Nobody chooses.** The B-vs-B' choice is mechanical, fixed at this merge, before the look.
   - **Disclosed.** On a PASS the counts are correlated with the read's outcome (more attempts, more fills). Because P0 can change nothing given a PASS, that correlation cannot select anything. The P0 dates lie inside the read window. P18's clock hours, from 2026-11-04T00:00Z on, are disjoint from it.
10. **Unchanged:** the promotion gate and every threshold (n >= 100; >= 5 UTC dates with a majority positive; CI90 lower bound > 0, 1,000 draws, seed 1, 5th percentile; ex-top-3 > 0; flat 15% and pressure-fail at slope scale 1); §2's one read per `bot_id`, its deciding cell and its outcome-blind read instant; Amendment 3 item 1 and Amendment 4 item 2 for every other book, and for P18 in every part except the P0 source; Amendments 5 to 19; every registered book; ruling Q1 items 1 to 6 (no live DBC process, probe, decision log or credit meter before LOOK CONSUMED; no heavy P18 job on research-0 from 2026-10-31T22:00Z until the read ends) and ruling Q3, Q4 and Q6; every seal.
11. **Disclosure.** In view: DEC-031 through Amendment 18 at origin/main `a31d9864`; QP-RULING-1026-p33-p18; `docs/research/dbc-p18/DESIGN.md`; PR #950 STAGING.md at `2ac6fca0`; `docs/research/dbc-m10-forward/REGISTRATION.md` and `AMENDMENT-1.md`; READ-HARDENING.md (searched only); `tools/dbc_fwd_read_am1.py` lines 1 to 175 and `tools/dbc_screen.day_of` (to name the field); `scripts/research/dbc-m10-read-job.sh` (searched only); the P18 readiness desk audit of 2026-10-27 (manager scratch, not in git). Amendment 19 (PR #1064) merged after this text was first written and was not in view; this amendment does not touch it. **Not opened:** `fwdv1/` and every DBC hour in [2026-10-25T00Z, 2026-11-01T00:10Z); the read's output directory (not listed); every DBC hour in [2026-10-17, 2026-10-24) (SPENT); `/data/mal/meta-capture/` (not listed); every Q50 and HV4 output; any P&L file. No #890 test file was run. No count, job, replay or RPC call was made. **Not measured by this amendment:** the read's verdict; any attempt count; the live P18 rate; the Helius feed (never probed); the gap between the proxy and the live count (dry-score items 2 and 4 measure it).

### Amendment 18, Erratum 1 (written 2026-10-30T15:41:51Z from `date -u` on mal-research-0; records only; text copied verbatim from the quant-proof ruling `ops/manager18/QP-RULING-1030-s1-memory.md` (sha256 `b8b17a4527c55963ced9aa4fb1df154aa51605354a779fce2f54387d221f1aff`) sections 1.1 and 1.3; before the registration of `h5v1q50-boostfloor-s1`, before its Amendment 6 smoke and before any outcome of the book it covers exists; no condition depends on any Q50 outcome or verdict (Amendment 17 item 3); outcome-blind; structure-only; status: DRAFT until merge, QP OK on the exact head and a reviewer verdict; merges before or with the code of PR #1086, never after it, and before the 2026-11-04 registration)
DEC-031 Amendment 18, Erratum 1: in Amendment 18 item 8, the words "every new process (the smoke, the successor shadow) has its own `MemoryMax`, and before each start `systemctl show user-1002.slice -p MemoryCurrent` plus the sum of all running and new `MemoryMax` values must fit the slice; otherwise the start waits (a slip under Amendment 17 item 2, never a reason to start uncapped)" are replaced by: "every new process (the smoke, the successor shadow) has its own finite `MemoryMax`. Before each start, on mal-fast-0, **P + S + N + M ≤ L** must hold, where: **P** = `memory.current` − `inactive_file` − `active_file` + `file_mapped` of `/sys/fs/cgroup/user.slice/user-1002.slice` (its `memory.current` and `memory.stat`, read once; reclaimable page cache is not counted; anon, shmem, kernel and mapped file pages are); **S** = the sum of the finite `MemoryMax` of every running unit of uid 1002's user manager (`systemctl --user list-units --type=service,scope --state=running`), the start job's own unit included; **N** = the new process's `MemoryMax` (1500M = 1572864000 for the successor shadow); **M** = 2147483648 (2 GiB, margin for the growth of the allowlisted uncapped units); **L** = the slice's `MemoryMax` (MemTotal if it has none). A running user unit with no finite `MemoryMax` refuses, unless it is on this allowlist: `init.scope`, `dbus.service`, `gpg-agent.service`, `claude-remote.service`, `miscusi-agent.service`, and units matching `tmux-spawn-*.scope`. An allowlisted unit is not summed: it counts at its current use inside P, and the check prints its `MemoryCurrent` and `MemoryPeak`. Each of these refuses: an unreadable `memory.current` or `memory.stat`; a missing or non-numeric field; an unreadable or empty unit list. The check is read-only: it never writes `memory.reclaim` or any cgroup file. Otherwise the start waits (a slip under Amendment 17 item 2, never a reason to start uncapped). Basis: `ops/manager18/QP-RULING-1030-s1-memory.md`."

**Ops line (ruling section 1.3; part of this erratum, not a code check).** While a shadow of Q50, s1 or Q60 runs on mal-fast-0, other work on mal-fast-0 that is expected to use more than 256 MB runs only as a capped unit (a MiScusi job, or `systemd-run --user --scope -p MemoryMax=…`) of at most 1500M, and only after `sh scripts/ops/q50s1-shadow-start.sh --check-memory` exits 0 immediately before it starts. Its N term (1500M) then covers that unit. Nothing heavy runs inside an allowlisted uncapped scope (a Claude Code tmux session).

Unchanged by this erratum: the promotion gate and its thresholds; every other word of Amendment 18 (S\*, the clock, the window, the read rule, the A3 items); Amendment 17; every running book. No book has cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here is a profit claim.

Ruling section 1.2 (an Amendment 19 erratum for `h5v1q60-boostfloor-a1`) is not entered: the manager withdrew Q60 before its registration (decision notebooked as n_F60AKfUlWM41kw, under QP-RULING-1030-power-n R4 (a); its amendment line is owed separately). Amendment 19 item 8 is left as written. If Q60 or another H5 sibling is ever proposed again, its own amendment sets its fit check under this erratum's rule and counts every H5 process that can run at the same time.

1. **HV4 successor policy (Amendment 17 pattern; policy only).**
   1. **The commitment.** Whenever and however the nine HV4 books end, **all nine** successors are to be registered, one per arm, never a subset. An end is a screen read of any verdict, a confirmation read, the NOT_DECIDABLE end at 1794096000000, or any other end. The successors are:
      - `bot_id`s `hv4s1-CON-L`, `hv4s1-CON-F`, `hv4s1-CON-P`, `hv4s1-CON-X`, `hv4s1-CON-R`, `hv4s1-BIG-L`, `hv4s1-BIG-F`, `hv4s1-BIG-X`, `hv4s1-BIG-R`. Each is the successor of the `hv4-` book of the same arm.
      - **Same book definition.** `frozen_sha` `9ed4c5ff63935696b2a89150e13565e123047754` (HV4's). Also HV4's arms, prompts, reviewer instructions, model ids (`["claude-haiku-5-5"]` on the L, F, P and R arms, `[]` on the X arms), sampling settings, lane G only (`lane_h` false), deciding stake 0.25 and deciding cell, stack config pin (sha256 `1e611a5a636dfe6bd563ab5258f6b498cdb8906500ee8bf2f0264a2621621986`) and `--feed-delay-slots 7`. No HV4 entry counts from 1794096000000 (2026-11-08T00:00:00Z) on (Amendment 15 item 1 cut-off), and HV4's confirmation ends there too (Amendment 15 item 3). So the windows are disjoint (Amendment 3 item 5: no second frozen copy inside the screen or confirmation window), with one day, [2026-11-08T00Z, 2026-11-09T00Z), counted by neither book. That day keeps the two processes apart. HV4's segment-2 job #1562 runs to 2026-11-08T13:05:00Z, and its start guard (`/data/mal/hunt-1008/push-1016/hunt20/haiku-v4/t0-1025/E4-runner-seg2.sh:25`) refuses a resume while any other `hv4_runner.py` process is alive. If a pre-clock item (item 1.4) is late, the clock is the first 00:00Z after the last item (a slip), never an earlier one.

The runner's END is the window end plus 13 h 05 min, HV4's run-out: **2026-11-23T13:05:00Z (1795439100000)** for the 11-09 clock.
      - **P0 (Amendment 4 item 2.1).** HV4's own P0 is reused: the A2 count at the same sha `9ed4c5ff` on the 3 full closed UTC days 2026-10-21 to 10-23 (registration record section 3a; lowest arm 230.667 would-be attempts a day, an upper bound; no day at 0). It meets item 2.1 literally: 3 full closed days at the registration-candidate sha, before the clock. P0 decides only the floor and H, and H = 14 for any rate of at least 150/14 = 10.714285714285714 a day. No fresh P0 is run for a clock at or before 2026-11-10T00:00Z: its 3 days before the clock would include HV4-counted hours in [2026-10-25, 2026-11-08), and a structure count of the HV4 rule there is HV4 attempt-count information under HV4's seal. **Disclosed:** the reused days are 17 days before the clock. For a clock slipped past 2026-11-10T00:00Z, a new structure-only P0 is pre-committed here. H is then recomputed from it.

      **No HV4 screen read, confirmation read, verdict (PASS, FAIL, NOT_DECIDABLE or unread), outcome, P&L, fill, attempt count, trade count or read instant is ever a reason to register or not register a successor, to register some and not others, or to pick a successor's sha, clock, stake, H, window or read.**
   2. **Identity at a fixed sha (Amendment 7 pattern).** The frozen code names its books `hv4-<arm>` (`bot_id`, tools/hv4_runner.py:103-104@9ed4c5f).
